patippurai
innūliṉ mūla āciriyar aṟiñar kilperṭ cilēṭṭar 1915 - 1922il tamiḻnāṭṭil (aṉṟaiya ceṉṉaimākāṇat teṉpakuti) ceṉṉaippalkalaik kaḻakattiṉ intiyap poruḷātārat tuṟaiyiṉ mutal pērāciriyarāy,talaivarāyp paṇiceytavar. poruḷātāram maṭṭumiṉṟi, varalāṟu,moḻiyiyal, camūkaviyal, paṇpāṭu mutaliya paltuṟaikaḷilum vallavar.
tamiḻ - tamiḻar āyvai mēṟkoḷvārkkup peritum utavuvatākiyainnūl, empatippakam ivvāṇṭu veḷiyiṭum putiya nūlkaḷil oṉṟākum.itaṉai empatippaka nūlāciriyarkaḷuḷ oruvarākiya aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉcemmaiyāka, nayamākat tamiḻākkiyuḷḷār.
intiyat tuṇaikkaṇṭattiṉ nākarikattil, tirāviṭap (tamiḻiya)paṇpāṭṭu k kūṟuka ḷ aṉaittut tuṟaikaḷilum mukkāṟ pakutikkumatikamāka (twelve annas in the rupee) uḷḷaṉa eṉpār cunītikumārcaṭṭarji. itaṉaik kaṭanta 90āṇṭu (1924-2014) kālamcār palatuṟaiāyvukaḷ mēlum valuvāka niṟuviyuḷḷatai, pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya“cilēṭṭariṉ muṭivukaḷ kaṭanta 90 āṇṭukaḷil mēlum valuvaṭaintuḷḷamai”paṟṟiyatāṉa ceytikaḷai nūṉmukattil kāṇalām.
“intiya nākarika uruvākkattil tirāviṭa (tamiḻiya) moḻi pēcunarpaṅku: maṟupārvai tēvai” eṉpatu kuṟitta aṟiñar āntri ep. jōparkkuammaiyār 1990 kaṭṭurai (innūliṉ pak: 119-165) innūṟ karuttukaḷaivirivāka ātarippataik kāṇalām. tamiḻ-tamiḻar āyvai mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷukkup perum payaṉtaravalla innūlai veḷiyiṭuvatilperumaippaṭukiṟōm.

iṇaippu :
intiya nākarika uruvākkattil tirāviṭa moḻi

kilperṭ slēṭṭar(1924)
muṉṉurai
1915-ām āṇṭiṉ kōṭaip paruvattiṉpōtu ceṉṉaippalkalaik kaḻakattiṉ āṭcik kuḻu eṉṉai intiyap poruḷātarattuṟaiyiṉ (Indian Economics)talaimaikkup parinturaittateṉṟuaṟivikkappaṭṭatum, nāṉ tamiḻ paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. appoḻutu‘i n ti ya nāka ri ka t ti ṉ mūla mu ta l āriyaccārpāṉatu’ eṉṟu iṉṟuintiyāvilum airōppāvilum paraviyuḷḷa kōṭpāṭṭiṉ uḷḷārnta
valuvil eṉakku mutaṉmutal aiyamēṟpaṭṭatu. ceṉṉai vanta piṉ(nakaramaipput tiṭṭa vakaiyil ceṉṉaiaracāṅkattiṉ aṟivuraiyāḷarāyirunta). ec.vi. laṅkāsṭaraik kaṇṭu kalanturaiyāṭiyatil,ivvaiyaṅkaḷ piṉṉum vaḷarntaṉa. avar eṉakku mikac cuvaiyāṉa palatakavalkaḷait tantatuṭaṉ, civakaṅkai paṟṟiya viyattaku niḻaṟpaṭat tokutioṉṟaiyum eṉakkuk kāṭṭiṉār.
2. 1 92 2 iḷavēṉil va rai nāṉ teṉṉin ti yāvil taṅ ki yakālattil tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ tōṟṟam, intiya nākarikattil ataṉpaṅku paṟṟiyum, eṉ uḷḷam ōyātu cintittu vantatu. ittakaiya ārvamkoṇṭirunta piṟarai nāṉ kaṇṭāl, avarkaḷuṭaṉum vātiṭuvēṉ.ivvāṟutāṉ innūl karuttukkaḷ eṉ eṇṇattil uruvāyiṉa. 1922-ilnāṉ iṅkilāntukkut tirumpic cellum varum varai eliyaṭ cumit, periākiyavarkaḷ nūlkaḷum māntar iṉattōṟṟa ārāycci, tolliyaltuṟaikaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭup paravutaṟ koḷkai cārnta nūlkaḷum eṉakkutteriya varavillai. intiyāvukku eṉ poruḷātārattuṟai cārnta nūlkaḷtavira maṟṟap putiya nūlkaḷ eṉṉai aṭaiyavillai.
3. innūliṉ mutal nilai varaivai intiyāvil 1920-ileḻutiṉēṉ. ataṉai niṟaivu paṭuttuvataṟkāṉa tūṇṭutal tantavarkaḷāṉa ṭākṭar ī.ī. pavar(Power), pērāciriyar puḷūr (Fleure)ākiyōrukkuk kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. mēlum taṭṭaccuppaṭi accuttiruttappaṭikaḷai vācittu putuk karuttukkaḷait tantutaviya pērāciriyarkaḷpuḷūr, eliyaṭ cumit(Elliott smith) ākiyōrukkum nāṉ pēraḷavu kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. attakaiya pala karutturaikaḷuḷ cila maṭṭumēavarkaḷuṭaiya karuttukaḷ eṉa teḷivākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.4. mutal iyal cārnta puḷūr kuṟippil oru ciṟu ceytiyai nāṉ

intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷ
muḻumaiyāka ēṟka iyalavillai. paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ paraviya vakaiyili n tiyāvaiyum mesepoṭṭēmiyāvaiyum avar iṇaittapōtilum, kaṭalvāṇikattai viṭat tarai vāḻi vāṇikamē mukkiya kāraṇameṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. mottattil kaṭal neṟiyē mukkiyattuvammikkatāyirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ. itu ōraḷavuakkāla nilaimai kāraṇamākavē āyiṉum, “kaṭaṟkarai piṉ vāṅkiyapiṉṉar eritu nakariṉ vāḻvu ciṟuttup pōyviṭṭatu” eṉṟa varalāṟṟucceytiyaiyum ātāramākak koṇṭatu eṉatu muṭivu. ki.mu. 3000-2000 kālaaḷavil taraivaḻi vāṇikattai viṭak kaṭal vāṇikat toṭarpēmukkiya māyiruntatu eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalāviṭiṉum ataṟkēmutaṉmai taralām. pāracīkakkuṭāvaik kaṭantapiṉ, kaṭalvaḻippātai iraṇṭākap pirintu mēṟku nōkkiya kiḷai arēpiyāviṉ kaṭaṟkaraivaḻiyē ceṉṟatu. aṭutta kiḷai kiḻakku teṟkākavum intiyak kaṭaṟkaraiōramākac ceṉṟatu. iraṇṭu kiḷaikaḷumē camamukkiyattuvam vāyntirukkalām.
5. ekiptukkum intiyāvukkum iṭaiyilāṉa kaṭalvaḻit toṭarpukku puḷūr avarkaḷaiviṭa eliyaṭ cumittaip piṉ paṟṟi atika mukkiyattuvam tarukiṟēṉ. avarkaḷiṭam iruntu caṟṟu vēṟupaṭṭu ivvāṇikattoṭarpu intiyappaṇpāṭṭai am mēṟṟicai nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟirukkalām eṉṟu karutuvatilum, āriyar varavukku muṉṉarēintiyāvil valuvuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkivanta tirāviṭa nākarikam ammēṟṟicai nāṭukaḷukkum paraviyirukkavāyppuṇṭu ākaiyālataṟkum atika mutaṉmai taravēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu nilaippāṭu ākum.


nūṉmukam
[1924 - 2014]: toṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷil ittuṟai āyvilnikaḻntuḷḷavaṟṟiṉ curukkam. [mēlum pala takavalkaḷuṭaṉ itaivirivāka viḷakkuvatu iṇaippil kāṇum virivāṉa āntrē ep. jōperkukaṭṭurai]
iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum nākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavai uṇmaiyil tamiḻar, tamiḻiya nākarikak kūṟukaḷē “intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi - paṇpāṭu ākiyavaiyē”
- es. ē. ṭailar 1973
All of Indian civilisation is built on an underlying base ofDravidian language and culture - S.A. Tyler (1973) India an Anthropological Perspective
iṅkilāntiluḷḷa palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟiya piṉṉarāṅkilēya aṟiñar, kilperṭ cilēṭṭar 1915-23 kāla aḷavil ceṉṉaippalkalaikkaḻakattil intiyap poruḷiyal Indian Economics tuṟaiyaiāyvuttuṟaiyākat toṭaṅki nilaip paṭuttiṉār. 1916 - 17 il cumār pattuteṉṉintiyak kirāmaṅkaḷil(pala iṉṟaiya tamiḻnāṭṭil) poruḷiyal kaḷaāyvu ceytu 1918 il Some South Indian Villages eṉṉum āyvu nūlaiveḷiyiṭṭār. [piṉṉar atēkirāmaṅkaḷil pi.jē. tāmas mīṇṭum kaḷaāyvu ceytu 1940il aṉṟaiya nilaiyai viḷakkum āyvai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.]
2. ivar paltuṟai vallunar. iṅkilāntiṟku pōṉa piṉṉar 1931il SevenShakespeares eṉṟa nūlai eḻutiyavar [es. muttaiyā THEHINDU nāḷitaḻ 20.2.2012 kaṭṭurai]. poruḷiyal kuṟittu aintāṟunūlkaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār. 1923 il ivar eḻutiya intiyap paṇpāṭṭiltirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak ] kūṟukaḷ The Dravidian element in Indian Culturenūl (āṅkilattil 192 pakkaṅkaḷ) pukaḻpeṟṟatākum. (i) intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak (tamiḻiyak) kūṟukaḷpaṟṟiya slēṭar 1924 muṭivukaḷaik kaṭanta 90 āṇṭukaḷiṉpaltuṟai āyvukaḷ mēlum valuvāka ātarikkum nilaimai
3. 1856 - 1980 kāla kaṭṭattilēyē kālṭuvel maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, ñāṉap pirakācar, ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar mutaliyōr niṟuviya tirāviṭa



moḻikkuṭumpat toṉmaiyai iṉṟu paltuṟai aṟiñarum (scholars ofvarious disciplines of Sciences and Humanities like Human Palaentology,Human Palaeogenetics, Comparative and Historical Linguistics - includingNostratic and Mother Tongue studies,) ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. atumaṭṭumallatamiḻiya moḻi pēcunariṉ toṉmai, intiyāvilum teṉṉintiyāvilum iṉṟaikku muṉṉar (i.mu = Before Present; B.P.) 50000 -10000 varaic celkiṟatu. muntu tamiḻiyattiṉ toṉmaiyāṉatu intōairōppiyam mutaliya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ tāyāṉa nāstirātik ,yūrēṣiyāṭik varaic celkiṟatu. kāṇka: Chronology of the Major partof the world’slanguages accepted by scholars of Nostratic and MotherTongue studies including A.R. Bomhard Reconstructing Proto - NostraticBrill. Leiden (2008)
4. tamiḻiya moḻikkuṭumpattiṉ toṉmai teṉṉintiyāvilumintiyāvilum imu. 70000 - 50000 aḷavukku muntiyatu eṉpatai iṉṟunam kālattil āṇittaramāka niṟuvi varupavarkaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉavaramerikka aṟiñar stīpaṉ hilyar leviṭ (1943-) mēlai intōairōppiya moḻikaḷilum, tol intō - airōppiyam, nāstirātikiṉṟum kāṇappaṭum pala tamiḻiya moḻiyiyal kūṟukaḷai niṟuviyuḷḷār.ñā. tēvanēyap pāvaṇariṉ moḻiyiyal nōkkāṉatu ēṟkat takkateṉanilaināṭ ṭi va ru m mu ta ṉmaiyāṉa mēlai moḻi yi ya la ṟi ña r . i va ramerikkāvil niyūyārkkil vāḻpavar. avar (International Journalof Dravidian Linguistics IJDL) 2007 kaṭṭuraiyil terivittatukuṟippiṭattakkatu:-
āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaip paṟṟi āyvuceyta aṟiñar ammakkaḷiṉmoḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumē toṭarpuḷḷaṉa eṉak kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.uṟavumuṟai (Kinship) pūmarāṅ (vaḷaitaṭi) payaṉpāṭu ākiyavaiyum ammakkaḷukkum tirāviṭarukkum uḷḷa toṭarpaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 6000ai oṭṭiulakeṅkum kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu nilappakutikaḷiṉ parappu curuṅkiya poḻutu ulakiṉpiṟa pakuti makkaḷuṭaiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṭam iruntu tuṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar āstirēliyappaḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ. avarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviliruntu āstirēliyā vukkup pulampeyarntatu ēṟattāḻa 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irukkalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar
āstirēliya aṟiñar. itiliruntu kuṟaintatu 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) moḻipēcunar intiyāvil iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.inta
(“tirāviṭar ēṟṟam”) kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉṉintiyāvi
liruntu vaṭanāṭu celkiṉṟaṉar; piṉṉar aṅkiruntu pāracīkattiṟkum atait tāṇṭippiṟa nilappakutikaḷukkum celkiṉṟaṉar. anta piṟa nilappakutikaḷil mūla tirāviṭamoḻik kuṭumpattiliruntu pirintu urālik, altāyik, intō-airōppiyam ākiyamoḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. intō-airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattukku
tirāviṭam tāyā? tamakkaiyā? eṉpatai iṉṟaiya nilaiyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamākakkūṟa iyalātu.”
2013 cūṉ IJDL kaṭṭuraiyil leviṭ muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu: tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) moḻikaḷil nām kāṇum oliyaṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷ; oru oliyaṉukkuppakaramāka iṉṉoru oliyaṉaip peytu poruḷ māṟṟam , putupporuḷterivippatu ivaiyellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil kaḻipaḻaṅkālattukkumuṉṉarē iruntataik kāṇalām. ammāṟṟaṅkaḷai nāstirātiknilaiyilēyē muntu tirāviṭattiṭam iruntu ki.mu. 45000kku muṉṉarpeṟṟuviṭṭa (intō - airōppiyam uḷḷiṭṭa) piṟa nāstirātikamoḻip peruṅkuṭumpa moḻikaḷil (Indo European and other languagefamilies coming under Nostratic Macrofamily ) iṉṟum nilaipeṟṟuḷḷataik kāṇalām.
5. piṉlāntu nāṭṭaṟiñar hāṉu pāṉu akasti hakōlā tamatu2009 / 2011 nūl Lexical Affinities Between Tamil and Finnish (A Contributionto Nostratic studies from the angle of close Genetic affinitiesbetween the Dravidian and Uralic Languagefamilies ), nūlil 765 +373 colloppumaikaḷai parō - emeṉō (1983) ṉunuṉucu maṟṟumcentamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal pērakara mutali (1985 - 2007) ivaṟṟaiyumpayaṉpaṭutti āyvu ceytu tantuḷḷār. innūlil hakōlā veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa muṭivukaḷ varumāṟu:(i) 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉatu ñāla mutaṉ moḻi (MotherTongue of Man ) anta ñāla mutaṉ moḻikku aṭutta nilaiyil tōṉṟiya moḻipperuṅ kuṭumpaṅkaḷil (Macrofamily)  oṉṟāṉa Pre - Proto Nostratic  (tolnāstrātik) mutaṉ mutalil teṉṉintiyāvil nilavi yirukkalām. ulakil iṉṟuḷḷatamiḻiyam, intō airōppiyam, cemittiyam, urāl - altāyik moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ



 (maṟṟuma ki.mu. 3000 aḷavil vaḻaṅkiya cumēriyam, elām ākiya moḻikaḷ) ivaṟṟiṉtāykku moḻiyiyalāḷar cūṭṭiyuḷḷa peyar nāstirātik / yurōciyāṭik eṉpatākum.sasṭāṉpōrṭu palkalaik kaḻakattil 2000 aktōparil patrirāju kiruṣṇamūrttiyōṭu pēcumpoḻutu jōcap hec. kirīṉperku kūṟiyatu: “tirāviṭa
moḻikkuṭumpam yurēciyāṭikkiṉ makaḷ alla; cakōtari yākattāṉ iruntirukka
muṭiyum” Bh. Krishnamurthy (2003) The Dravidian languages.]
	“(ii) 1000 ((Before Present) BP) kṣaña ai oṭṭi tol - nāstirātik pēcunarteṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭakku nōkkip paraviya piṉṉar (tol tirāviṭa - tamiḻiya -moḻiyiṉar intiyā aḷavil niṟka) nāstirātikkiṉ ēṉaiya piriviṉar maiyak kiḻakku- maiya āciyap pakutiyil 10000 - 8000 BP kāla aḷavil uṭaṉuṟainta piṉṉar, cilaāyiram, āṇṭukaḷil cumēriyam, intō - airōppiyam, urāl - altāyik,cemittiyam ākiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pirintu āciyāvil pala pakutikaḷukkum, ekiptumutaliyavaṟṟukkum paraviṉa.
“ (iii) 7,000 BP (= kimu 5000) yiliruntu intiyā muḻuvatum (cintu - pañcāpuṭpaṭa) tamiḻiya moḻi pēciyavarkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar.
“(ie) maiyakkiḻakku - maiya āciyap pakutiyil ki.mu. 6000 - 4000 aḷavilvāḻnta “intō - airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpa mūlamoḻiyai (Proto - Indo – European)pēciyavarkaḷiṭamiruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pirinta intō irāṉiyap piriviṉoru uṭkiḷaiyākiya “vēta - camskiruta” moḻi pēcunar kimu 2500 - 1500 kālaaḷavil ciṟu eṇṇikkaiyil vaṭamēṟku intiya (cintu - pañcāp) pakutikku vantuaṅku tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) moḻi pēcunaruṭaṉ kalantu viṭṭaṉar.”
6. intap piṉpulattil teṉ āciyāvil kaḻi paḻaṅkālattil orupotuvāṉa mūtātai moḻi iruntirukka vēṇṭum. taṟpōtaiya māntaiṉam (cumār 1 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar) ulakeṅkum paravattoṭaṅkiya kāla kaṭṭamē toltirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālamākalām. (It may be a question of a very ancient common substratum inSouth Asia, pre -Dravidian going back even to the original peopling ofthe world) v‹wh® Colin P. Masica (Paper in The Year Book - for 2001of south Asian languages and Linguistics; New Delhi; Sage Publications)
7. (i) iṉṟu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa 700 kōṭi maṉitarkaḷumē (atāvatuintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷtirāviṭar, intō airōppiyar, maṅkōliyar, cemittiyar, nīkrōvar,amerikka intiyar ākiya aṉaivarumē) hōmō cēpiyaṉcu (HomoSapiens or Anatomically Modern Humans)eṉṉum orē iṉattaiccārntavarkaḷ. ivviṉam āppirikkāvil iṉṟaikku oṉṟarai laṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vāḻnta orē tāyiṭam allatu kuḻuviṭam iruntutōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟaiya aṟiviyalāḷar aṉaivarum ēṟṟa muṭivu.kāṇka[Nature (London) 3 May 2012 Vol. 485 pak.25; maṟṟum Map onp.11 of Cyril Aydon (2009) A brief history of Mankind: 1,50,000 years ofHuman history (London; Constable and Robinson] eṉiṉum ēṟattāḻamaṉitaṉai yotta ‘muṉmānta’ (Hominid) iṉaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 48 laṭcam āṇṭukaḷil tōṉṟic cila pala laṭca āṇṭu vāḻnta piṉṉar muṟṟilum aḻintoḻintuviṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ hōmō erakṭacu iṉṟaikku muṉṉarBefore Present (i.mu. 17 laṭcam - ki.mu 50000) eṉṟa iṉamum aṭaṅkum.atu maṭṭumē āppirikkāvai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷilumparaviyiruntatu . krōmakṉāṉ maṉitaṉ, pīkiṅ maṉitaṉ, īṭalparkmaṉitaṉ, cāvaka maṉitaṉ, attirampākkam pāsil maṉitaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ(cumār 3 laṭcam āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vāḻntavarkaḷ) inta hōmōerakṭcu vakaiyaic cārntavarkaḷē. airōppāvil 40000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ varai vāḻntu piṉṉar aṭiyōṭu aḻintu pōṉa niyāṇṭartal(Neandarthal) (kimu 5 laṭcam - 30000) iṉamum avvāṟē.
(ii) viññāṉikaḷ mēlum niṟuviyuḷḷatu: iṉṟaikku 70,000 -50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarttāṉ āppirikkāvai viṭṭu taṟkāla māntaiṉam (AMH)pulam peyarntu ulakiṉ piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷukkup paraviyatueṉpatākum. avvāṟuparavumuṉṉarē māntaṉ mutaṉmoḻi Mother Tongueof Man uruvākiviṭṭatueṉṟu varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyalāḷar uṭpaṭa palatuṟaiaṟiñarum karutukiṉṟaṉar.H.H. Hock and B.D. Joseph (2009: II Revised edition) “Languagehistory, Language change and Language relationships (Historical andComparative linguistics”Mouten de Gruyter: Berlin. p 474: “Languageoriginated about 1,00,000 -- 50000 Before Present.... by way of gesturalshift to oral channel... vocal sounds at first were emphasisive attachkilperṭ slēṭṭar ments” (cuṭṭukaḷiṉ mutaṉmai paṟṟi kālṭuvel, ñāṉappirakācar aṉṟēkarutiyatum atuvē)...
 	(iii) avvāṟu āppirikkāvai viṭṭup peyarntu paraviya māntappiriviṉaril mukkiyamāṉa oru pirivu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāka (teṉṉintiyāeṉpatu akkāla kaṭṭattil kaṭalōra nilappakutiyāka irunta kaṇṭattiṭṭuContinental Shelf pakutiyum cērntatu) ceṉṟu cilar teṉṉintiyāvilēyēnilaittiṭa; vēṟu cilar āstirēliyā varai kaṭalōra nilappakuti vaḻiyākametumetuvāka, atāvatu oru talaimuṟaikku oṉṟiraṇṭu mail vītam, ceṉṟaṭaintaṉar. intiyak karai cārnta kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyil atāvatu karaiyōrak kaṭaṟpakutiyil (Continental Shelf) āḻkaṭalakaḻāyvu ceytāl itu paṟṟiya tolliyal cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaikkalāmeṉpar piḷemiṅ (2004). āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷakkaṇṭattil imu 50000 mutal uḷḷaṉa.
 (iv) iṉṟaiya tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ avvāṟu iṉṟaikku 70000-50000 āṇṭu muṉṉar āppirikkāvai viṭṭu nīṅki āstirēliyāvaraic ceṉṟaṭainta māntariṉ piṟaṅkaṭaikaḷ perumaḷavil uḷḷaṉareṉpatai maiṭakāṉṭiriyal DNA mānta marapaṇu āyvu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākaniṟuviviṭṭatu. avvāyvai mēṟkoṇṭa viññāṉikaḷ kuḻuvil maturaiyaiccārnta muṉaivar irāmacāmi piccappaṉum oruvar. 23.8.2007, 16.4.2008nāḷkaḷil avar ceytittāḷ nērkāṇalkaḷil terivitta ceytikaḷ varumāṟu:-
“āppi ri kkāvai viṭṭu iṉṟaikku 70000- 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar veḷiyēṟiya oru kuḻuviṉariṭam mai DNA aṭaiyāḷakkuṟiyīṭu M130 tōṉṟiyatu. iṉṟuḷḷa āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷtokaiyiṉaruḷ iraṇṭil oruvariṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu.
“maturaip pakutiyil uḷḷa makkaḷ palaruṭaiya mai DNA-vai āyvuceytatil avarkaḷil 5liruntu 7 naparkaḷiṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu eṉṟuteriya vantatu. aṉṟaiya kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti vaḻiyāka mānta iṉattiṉ orupirivu āppirikkāviliruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pulam peyarntatu eṉpataiinta āyvu meyppittatu (Proceedings of theNational Academy ofSciences; USA ; 2001) piriṭṭaṉ nāṭṭu marapaṇuviyalaṟiñar car vālṭarpāṭmar coṉṉatu pōl. “ulakil iṉṟuḷḷa makkaḷ aṉaivarum (700intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷkōṭi pērumē) āppirikkāviliruntu pulampeyarntu vantavarkaḷiṉpiṟaṅkaṭaikaḷē.”
	‘intiya makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum mai DNA aṭaiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭukaḷil mikat toṉmai vāyntatu M130 tāṉ. ataiviṭap paḻaimaiyāṉamai DNA kuṟiyīṭukaḷintiyāvil evariṭamum illai. āppirikkāvilikkāla mānta iṉam AMH uruvākiya piṉṉar akkaṇṭattai viṭṭumutalil pulam peyarntu intiyāvil mutalil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷiṉpiṟaṅkaṭaikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷaṉar eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
8. “(ki.mu 300 - ki.pi. 200 kālattaic cārnta) caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷtamiḻaka maṇṇilēyē tol varavākat tōṉṟiyavai eṉpataik kāṭṭumaṉaittu aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. enta nāṭṭiliruntō tamatu
moḻiyuṭaṉum ilakkiyattuṭaṉum tamiḻar tamiḻakattukku vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatākayātoru ātāramum annūlkaḷil illai eṉpataiyum itiliruntuuṇaralām.” eṉṟa cēviyartaṉi nāyakam (1953) muṭivu iṉṟu acaikkamuṭiyātatāka uḷḷatu. slēṭariṉ iyal 1il tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarmecapotāmiyā pakutiyiliruntu ki.mu. 3000kkuppiṉ intiyāvukkuḷnuḻaintu piṉṉart tamiḻnāṭu varaip piṉṉar kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉṟauṉṉippu iṉṟu aṭipaṭṭuviṭṭatu. māṟāka, mikap paḻaṅ kālattil (ki.mu.5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar) mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik karaiyiluruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiya mēṟkukkarai vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷālcintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu, naṇṇilakkarai nāṭukaḷ varai koṇṭucellappaṭṭatu. eṉṟu - tiruttantai hīrās toṉmai intō -naṇṇilakkarai nākarika āyvu (Studies in Indo Mediterranean Culture1953) nūlil niṟuviyuḷḷa muṭivaiyē iṉṟaiyap paltuṟai āyvukaḷumātarikkiṉṟaṉa.“tirāviṭa” moḻik kuṭumpamā? “tamiḻiya” moḻik
kuṭumpamā?
9. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamilian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟēkālṭuvel kālam varai vaḻaṅkiya peyarai avar tāṉ “tirāviṭa” moḻikkuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccait tiripuvaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil “tamiḷa”, taṇṭi eḻutiya   kilperṭ slēṭṭar avanticuntari katai yil “tramiḷa” eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvākukuṭimara piṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum ‘tamiḷa’ tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉarkumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantiravārttikattil
“tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva”(appaṭi tirāviṭa (=tamiḻ) maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil)eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ-tamiḷa-tramiḷa-tramiṭa-traviṭa-tirāviṭa eṉṟu
māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci. parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyai acciṭṭapoḻutu apattamāka,
“āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva”eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻaikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉavācakattai kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō (Linguistic Survey ofIndia: 1906) pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟai pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42ām tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. (kuññīṇṇirājā Annals of Oriental Research tokuti28 (1979) kaṭṭuraiyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla, parṉel uṭaiya tavaṟāṉa
vācakattait tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟi “tamiḻaiyum, teluṅkaiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār” eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar.)
10. “tirāviṭam” eṉṉum collil iruntu “tamiḻ” uruvāṉatueṉṟu kālṭuvel tavaṟākak kuṟippiṭṭatu paṟṟi avaraik kuṟai kūṟalollātu. akkālattil tamiḻ cārnta moḻiyiyal āyvukaḷ irunta nilaiavvaḷavutāṉ. āṉāl avarukkup piṉṉar 1887il kalittokai ppatippuraiyil ci.vai. tāmōtaram piḷḷai toṭaṅki, kamil culepil (JourNal of the Institute of Asian Madras IV-2: 1987) muṭiya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa moḻiyiyalaṟiñar aṉaivarum “tamiḻ” eṉṉum colliṉ tiripuṟṟavaṭivamē “tirāviṭam” eṉṟu āṇittaramāka niṟuviyuḷḷa nilaiyiliṉṟum “tirāviṭam” eṉṉum colliliruntu “tamiḻ” eṉṉum coluruvākiyatu eṉa kūṟikkoṇṭiruppatu aṟiyāmai.
11. toltirāviṭam (Proto-Dravidian) eṉṟu vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūlāyvāḷar mīṭṭuruvākkam ceyvaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala piḻaiyāy muṭikiṉṟaṉaeṉpataiyum, paḻantamiḻē itaṟkuk kaṭṭaḷaik kallākak koḷḷappaṭavēṇṭum eṉpataiyum pāvāṇar niṟuviviṭṭār. tamiḻiṉ tiripukaḷē piṟaintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ. paḻantamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭatāka tol tirāviṭam eṉpatu kaṟpaṉaiyē eṉpar pāvāṇar. ākavē tirāviṭam, tol tirāviṭameṉa kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉa vellām uṇmaiyil paḻantamiḻaiyē kuṟippatākauṇarka. eṉiṉum ulaka aḷavil “tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam” eṉpatu orukuṟiyīṭṭuc col ākiviṭṭa nilaiyil atai nām payaṉpaṭuttuvatiltavaṟillai. ataṉ uṇmaip poru ḷ ‘ ta mi ḻi ya moḻik ku ṭu mpam’eṉpatē.
12. ji.yū.pōp tirukkuṟaḷ āṅkilap peyarppai 1886il veḷiyiṭṭapoḻutu annūl muṉṉuraiyil kūṟiyatu:-
“(tamiḻē) teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ tāy ākum. teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpattait tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpam eṉṟu aḻaippatē cari eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ. akkuṭumpa moḻikaḷil etaṉai āyvuceyya vēṇṭumeṉ ṟālum tamiḻmoḻip piṉṉaṇiyil tāṉ āyvuceyya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu”
(i) aṇmai āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṉ toṉmai- āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷaiyoppa - teṉṉintiyāvilum intiyā vilum i.mu. (iṉṟaikku muṉṉar;Before Present) 50000 varaic celkiṟatu.
(ii) attakaiya toltamiḻiyak kuṭu mpattiliruntē intōairōppiyam, urāl, altāyk pōṉṟa moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pirintirukkavēṇṭum eṉa iṉṟu karutappaṭuvatālum, (teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam pōṉṟaēṉaiya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟak kālattai eppaṭip pārttālum 2000āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉark koṇṭu cella iyalātu eṉpatālum) tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpam eṉṟu aḻaippatē cari; eṉiṉummoḻiyiyalil kuṟiyīṭṭuccol ākiviṭṭa “tirāviṭa” moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟa peyarait toṭarntu payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭiyatākiṟatu.
 	13. el.vi. irāmacāmi aiyarum tamatu Educational Reviewcūlai 1928 kaṭṭuraiyil piṉvarumāṟu tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyai valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār:- “ ta mi ḻi ṉ  māperu m  coṟ ka ḷa ñ ci ya t ti l  u ḷ ḷa  coṟkaḷ  ovvoṉṟukkum teḷivāṉa tamiḻvēraik kāṇamuṭiyum; iṉṟu vaḻaṅkumtamiḻccoṟkaḷilum mikappala cila āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākat tūya kilperṭ slēṭṭar tamiḻ vaṭivattil vaḻaṅkivantuḷḷaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilēyē tamiḻtāṉmūlamoḻiyiṉ muḻuttaṉmaiyaip perum aḷavukku iṉṟum koṇṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻil kāṇum eḷimaiyāṉa teḷivāṉa vērkaḷiliruntutāṉcilapala (paṭimuṟaiyāṉa) māṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ēṉaiya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷai viḷakka iyalukiṟatu.
“eṉavē innilaiyil tamiḻccol etaṉaiyum tūya tamiḻ ōracai,īracai vēriṉ / vērkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil viḷa k ka iyalumpoḻutu (tamiḻil ivvāṟu viḷakkum poḻutu kiṭṭum arumaiyāṉa teḷivaiyumoḷiyaiyum kuḻappum vakaiyil) ac collukku tol tirāviṭa ProtoDravidian vaṭivattai uṉṉippatu utavākkarai vēlai. iṅkoṉṟumaṅkoṉṟumāka cila nērvukaḷil maṭṭumē piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uḷḷaciṟcila coṟkaḷai viḷakkuvataṟkutoltirāviṭaccol vaṭivaṅkaḷai uṉṉippatu tēvaippaṭum.”
Considering the fact that Tamil’s vast word-hoard couldbe traced to its own elementary native roots and further that someof the most ancient forms (so far as we know) have been handeddown to us in their pristine nature, Tamil represents within certainlimits the most conservative of Dravidian dialects. This viewreceives confirmation from the fact that many of the elementaryroots of Tamil are found (with modifications and alterations ofvarious degrees) in a large number of other dialects
“In this view then, if a Tamil word could be satisfactorilyexplained as being normally derived from a native Tamil root itself,or a combination of roots, it would be futile to constructproto-forms of words, obscuring the relationships of root andword in Tamil, unless indeed the affinities of Tamil words androots with those of other dialects demand it.”
- L.V. Ramaswami Iyer



 (ii) tol tamiḻiyac cintu nākarikam
14. 1923 ām āṇṭil eḻuti muṭittu 1924 il veḷivantatuslēṭariṉ innūl. āṉāl 1923kkup piṉṉart tāṉ car jāṉ mārṣaltalaimaiyil mokeñcotarō vilum harappāvilum akaḻvāyvukaḷintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷtoṭaṅkappaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭa muṭivukaḷ 1924 ceptempariltāṉ mārṣal Il-Lustrated London News kaṭṭuraiyil veḷiyiṭṭār. annākarikam tamiḻiya(tirāviṭa) nākarikam (ki.mu. 7000 - 1800); annākarika muttiraikaḷ tamiḻiyamoḻiyē eṉpatai virivāka pi. irāmanātaṉ (2012) tol tamiḻiyac cintunākarikam nūlil kāṇalām. cintunākaric ceytikaḷ vanta piṉṉar eḻutiyiruntāl intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak kūṟukaḷai mēlum valuvākattam nūlil cilēṭṭar niṟuviyiruppār.
(ii) hīrās tamatu 1953 “Studies in Proto Indo - MediterraneanCulture”nūliṉ aṟimukattil piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār:
mikap paḻaṅkālattil (ki.mu. 5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar)mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik karaiyil uruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiyamēṟkukkarai vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷāl cintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu,naṇṇilakkarai nāṭukaḷ varai koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu.
(intō āriya moḻi pēcunariṉ (ciṟu eṇṇikkaiyil) nuḻaivukkumuṉṉar) intiyāveṅkum tirāviṭamoḻi pēcunarē vacittu vantaṉar.(heviṭ 1889; hāl 1913, rikvētam upaniṣattukkaḷ kālattiliruntētirāviṭat tākkam (viṉaik koḷkai, maṟupiṟappu, yōkam) uḷḷatu. vēta,purāṇap pakutikaḷil pala paṇṭait tirāviṭa nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppukaḷē, (hīrācukku 22.11.1942 aṉṟu eḻutiya kaṭitattil vi.es. cuktaṅkaryutiṣṭiraṉ katai āriyar varavukkum rik vētattukkum muntiyatu eṉpatucariyāṉa karuttē’ eṉat terivittār.) nākarikamaṟṟa nilaiyil intiyāviṟkuḷ nuḻainta intō āriyar tamatu moḻiyai camskirutamāka(tiruntiya moḻiyāka) ākkikkoṇṭaṉar. (They converted their rudematter of -course speech -- a speech of shepherds and husbandmen -into a classical Sanskrit language)
tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar naṇṇilakkarai nāṭukaḷiliruntu intiyāviṟkum teṉintiyāviṟkum vantavarkaḷ allar. iṅkiruntu aṅkuceṉṟu piṉṉar ulakeṅkum nākarikattaip parappiyarvarkaḷ ivarkaḷē.ittuṟai āyvukaḷ niṟaivaṭaiyum poḻutu tirāviṭa nākarikamākiya cintunākarikam ulaka nākarikat tiṉ toṭṭil eṉpatu ēṟkappaṭum.“the Dravidians of India, after a long period of development inthis country, travelled westwards, and settling successively in the various lands, they found their way from Mesopotamia upto the Britishisles, spread theirrace -- afterwards named Mediterranean owing to theplace where they were known anthropologically -- through the west andmade their civilization flourish in two continents, being thus the originatorsof the modern world civilization. The Mediterranean nations of theancient world were racial off-shoots of the mighty proto - Indian race”[After the problems of decipherment of Indus script and those ofthe migration of Dravidian civilisation out of India to Elam, Sumeria andthe West are solved]India will be acknowledged as the cradle of humancivilization.
15. eṉ. lākōvari 1963il veḷiyiṭṭa tirāviṭar tōṟṟamum mēlnāṭukaḷum (Dravidian Origins and the West) eṉṉum nūl ciṟantamoḻiyiyal meymaikaḷai uṇarttuvatākum:-
“i) 6000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar intiyāviliruntu aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalvarai orē
mātiriyāṉa “pala col piṇippu oṭṭunilai” (Polysynthetic Suffixal)) moḻikaḷ, iṭaiyīṭuiṉṟip paraviyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ ōraḷavukku oṉṟupōl iruntaṉa.potuc col viḻukkāṭu moḻikku moḻi māṟupaṭṭu iruntirukkalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ,elāmaiṭ, cumēriyaṉ, harri, kākēciyaṉ, halti, pōṉṟavai orē moḻiyamaippuk koṇṭu oṉṟukkoṉṟu uṟavuṭaiyavai yākac caṅkilit toṭarpōla amaintiruntaṉa.
ii) “intō airōppiya moḻi pēcunar (ki.mu. 2000ai oṭṭi) airōppāvukkuḷkiḻakkiliruntu nuḻaiyumuṉṉar aṅku pēcappaṭṭu vanta moḻikaḷil oṉṟāṉa pāsku(yūskarā) iṉṟum pirāṉcukkum speyiṉukkum iṭaippa ṭṭa pireṉīs malaippakutiyil pēcappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. tol tirāviṭa moḻi inta pāsku moḻiyuṭaṉ maṭṭumiṉṟi intō airōppiya moḻikaḷukku muṉṉar airōppāvil pēcappaṭṭa toṉmoḻikaḷuṭaṉum uṟavuṭaiyatu.
iii) “immoḻikaḷ aṉaittum oṉṟukkoṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷavai;
orē potuvāṉa tāy moḻiyiṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷ eṉṟē kūṟalām.”- eṉ. lākōvari
 (iii) vētamoḻi camskirutattiṉ tāyākiyaintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷmuntu intō-airōppiyattilēyē kāṇum tirāviṭak(tamiḻiyak) kūṟukaḷ

17. intō-airōppiya moḻikaḷiliruntu kaṭaṉ peṟappaṭātavaiyum,eṉṟālum ammoḻikaḷiṉ tolpaḻaṅ kālat taṉmaikaḷaiyottaṉavākairuppaṉavum, āṉa piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟai i ṉ ṟu m tirāvi ṭa mo ḻi ka ḷkoṇṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpataiyum kālṭuvel niṟuviṉār. (1) “olippu eḷimai, iṉimaikkāka ‘na’viṉ payaṉpāṭu - kirēkka moḻiyiluḷḷatu pōla
(2) paṭarkkaiyiṭap pirati peyarkaḷilum viṉaic coṟka ḷilu m pā lvēṟupāṭu iruppatu - kuṟippāka potuppāl iruppatu.
(3) cuṭṭup piratip peyarkaḷilum, paṭarkkaip pirati peyarkaḷilum potuppālorumaiyaik kāṭṭa d, t payaṉpāṭu
(4) lattīṉil uḷḷatu pōla potuppāl paṉmaiyaik kāṭṭa a payaṉpāṭu
(5) cēymaic cuṭṭukku a; aṇmaic cuṭṭukku i payaṉpāṭu
(6) perciyaṉ moḻiyiṟpōla, perumpālum iṟanta kālattaik kāṭṭa t payaṉpāṭu
(7) vēril oli oliyaṉai iraṭṭittu cila coṟkaḷil, iṟanta kālattaik
kāṭṭutal
(8) viṉaic collil ōr uyireḻuttai nīṭṭi olittu viṉaiyālaṇaiyum
peyarkaḷai amaittal.
“
“Primitive underived Indo-Europeanisms discoverable in theDravidian languages (in current parlance: ‘proto-Indo European featuresderived from Dravidian”):-
1.The use of n, as in Greek to prevent hiatus.”
2. The existence of gender in the pronouns of the third person,
and in verbs, and in particular the existence of a neuter gender.
3. The use of d or t as the sign of the neuter singular of demonstrative
pronouns of the third person.
4. The existence of a neuter plural, as in Latin, in short a.
5. The formation of the remote demonstrative from a base in a; the proximate from the base in i.
6. The formation of most preterites, as in Persian by the addition of d.
7. The formation of some preterites by the reduplication of aportion of the root.
8. The formation of a considerable number of verbal nouns by
lengthening the vowel of the verbal root.”
- irāparṭ kālṭuvel

21. ñāṉappirakācar - tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi (iṉṟaiyanāstirātik āyvāḷar karuttum atuvē) ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉarē tolintō- airōppiyam pēcunar tol - tirāviṭam pēcunariṭamiruntu pirintuviṭṭaṉar. maiya āciya pulveḷikaḷil cila āṇṭukaḷ vāḻntaṉar, avarkaḷil cila kuḻuviṉar ki.mu. 4000-3000 aḷavil mēṟku nōkkiairōppāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. (avarkaḷiṭam piṉṉar uruvāṉavaiyē kirīk,lattīṉ, kelṭik, cermāṉik, cilāviya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷākum) vēṟucila kuḻukkaḷ kiḻakku-teṉkiḻakku nōkki iṭam peyarnta kālam
ki.mu. 2500ai oṭṭi ākum. avvāṟu kiḻakkē vanta kuḻuviṉar moḻikaḷil atāvatu intō āriya, irāṉiya moḻikaḷil paṇṭu (atāvatuki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar toltirāviṭa moḻiyiṉariṭamiruntu pirintakāla tti l ) u ṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟa tol tirāviṭa moḻikkūṟukaḷōṭu cērttu vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum appālum vāḻntuvanta tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarkaḷiṭam iruntu iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭamāka(a second dose of Dravidian features) rik vētattilēyē putitāka mēlumpala tamiḻiya moḻik kūṟukaḷ cērkkappaṭalāyiṉa. attakaiya iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭa nikaḻvukaḷai aṭutta pakuti (u) viḷakkukiṟatu.

23. ñālamutaṉmoḻi āyvāḷar meriṭ rūlaṉ taṉatu moḻikaḷiṉtōṟṟam - moḻikaḷiṉ koṭi vaḻi āyvu sṭāṉpōrṭu palkalaikkaḻakam,1994 nūliṉ pakkam 2777il kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:“perumpālāṉa moḻiyiyalaṟiñarkaḷ ottuk koḷḷāviṭiṉum allatuaiyappāṭṭuṭaṉ karutiṉum iṉṟuḷḷa moḻikaḷ aṉaittum (iṉṟuiṟantuviṭṭa āṉāl muṉṉar nilaviyataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭiyuḷḷamoḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa) orē ñāla mutaṉ moḻiyiliruntu tāṉ tōṉṟiṉaeṉpataṟku valuvāṉa ātāram uḷḷatu.”
annūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 277-366il 27 mukkiyamāṉa karuttukaḷukku palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷ “Global Etymologies” tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akkaruttukaḷukku ñāla mutaṉmoḻiyileṉṉa vērccol iruntirukkalām eṉṟu ūkikka atu muṉṉōṭiyākum. avaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟukku (ēṟattāḻa 17kku)t tamiḻc coṟkaḷēporuḷ potintaṉavāka vu m ñā la mu ta ṉ moḻiyiṉ vērccolvaṭivai viḷakkuvaṉavākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa. 24. i ṉ ṟaiya “ i n tu ” camayak kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭ ṭu muṟaikaḷ, meyyiyal ākiyavai muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭac) cārpuṭaiyavaiyē;āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavai alla eṉpatai 1900kku muṉṉar iruntēnallaṟiñar kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1903: mullaippāṭṭuārāycciyurai , 1923 tamiḻar nākarikam , 1941 tamiḻar matam); pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaaiyaṅkār (1929: History of the Tamils (1940tamiḻar camayam) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (1972: tamiḻar matam ) ākiyōr uṭpaṭa.āriya vētaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa, avaṟṟukku muntiya ‘tamiḻ nāṉmaṟaikaḷ’ iruntaṉa veṉṉum kōṭpāṭṭaiyum kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai(1927; The Madras Christian College Magazine; piṉṉar 1920(centamiḻccelvi) mutaliyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar.
2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟai - cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putuk kaṟkālak karuvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷnāṉku uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai ai. makātēvaṉ “murukuaṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār.akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000-1500āka irukkalām eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. “putukkaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orēmoḻiyaiyē pēciṉar eṉṟum ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē eṉṟum ikkaṇṭu piṭippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpar ai. makātēvaṉ.” (“The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu andthe Indus valley people shared the same language which can only beDravidian and not Indo-Aryan):1.5.2006 intu nāḷitaḻ.
25. iṉṟaiya vētamatak karuttukkaḷ - avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa, puttammutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭaiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇaippueṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉatalla. cintuveḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭaiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē,maṟumalarcciyē iṉṟaiya intu matam ākum. (The Hindu synthesis was less thedialectical reductionof orthodoxy and heterodoxy than the resurgenceof the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)”  eṉpar ṭailar (1973)
26. “paḻaiya upaniṭataṅkaḷil maṟupiṟavik koḷkai putumaiyāṉatākavum
cilarukku maṭṭum terinta maṟai poruḷākavum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu iruntatu. iruntum
piṉṉar akkoḷkai putta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka viraintu aṉaivarālum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu evvāṟu? enta makkaḷ camūkattiṉariṭam iruntu puttar tōṉṟiṉārō, avarkaḷiṭaiyē akkoḷkai iruntu irukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāṉ atu viraivilaṉaittu makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.”
- pirākiṅṭaṉ J.L. Brockington 1981: The Sacred thread
heṉric cimmar (1951) “vaṭa mēṟkiliruntu nuḻainta āriyarkaḷāl makatammutaliya kīḻtticai nāṭukaḷilirunta āriyarallāta mēṉmakkaḷ muḻumaiyāka iṭam
peyarntilar. attakaiya mēṉ makkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ valuviḻantu vīḻat toṭaṅkiṉa piṉ
muntaiya uḷḷūr āḷuṅ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ mīṇṭum valuppeṟṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭākacantirakupta mauriyaṉ ittakaiya kuṭumpattaic cārntavaṉ. puttarum avvāṟē.
- heṉric cimmar Heinrich Zimmer1951: Philosophies of India 
“intu camayattil yāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum uruva vaḻipāṭu intiyāvil irunta āriyarallātār vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷil iruntē pirāmaṇiyattukku vantatu”leviṉ - G.M. Bongard Levin (1986)
A complex study of Ancient India
 “mokañcotarō muttiraiyil kāṇappaṭupavar pōṉṟa (āriyarkaḷukkumuntaiya intiya) yōkikaḷ vantēṟikaḷ moḻiyaic cila kālattukkuḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭu tamatu camaya maṟai poruṭkaḷai (vētap pāṭalkaḷai iyaṟṟiya) pirāmaṇarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉarō”vālparṭ - Stanley Wolpert 1982: A new History of India.
intō āriya moḻi pēcunar iṅku vanta piṉṉarum vaṭa intiyāvil vāḻntutirāviṭa moḻi pēcivanta tol kuṭiyiṉar vantavarkaḷaiviṭap perum ṇṇikkaiyiliruntamaiyāl toṭarntu avaravar iṭattil iruntaṉar. vaṭa intiyap pakutiyilvāḻntu vanta tolkuṭiyiṉar intō āriya moḻi pēcuvōruṭaiya araciyal,
poruḷātāra, camayat tākkattiṉ kīḻ vantu cilakālam iru moḻi (tamatu toṉmoḻi yākiya tamiḻiya moḻi - maṟṟum āriyar moḻi) pēcuvōrāka iruntu nāḷaṭaivil tam toṉmoḻiyaik kaiviṭṭu āriyamoḻiyaik kaik koṇṭaṉar. vaṭa intiya makkaḷil perumpāṉmaiyiṉarāka irunta tirāviṭa moḻi pēcuvōr teṉ intiyāvukkuc ceṉṟu viṭṭatākak karuta iṭamillai. āriya varukaikku muṉṉar intiyā veṅkum paraviyirunta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ avvarukaikkup piṉ vaṭa intiyāvil nalivuṟṟaṉ eṉpatē naṭantirukka kūṭiyatu.
tāmas ṭirauṭmaṉ (1981) tirāviṭar uṟavu muṟairikvētam tokukkappaṭṭa kālam ki.mu. 1500 - 1300 ākam. intō āriyamoḻip paṭaippukaḷil mikappaḻaiyatu atuvē. avvētattilēyē intō - āriyamallātaṉavum, tirāviṭat taṉmai vāyntaṉavumāṉa pala kūṟukaḷ - oliyaṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷ,tirāviṭattiliruntu kaṭaṉpeṟṟa coṟkaḷ, iṭappeyarkaḷ, makkaṭ peyarkaḷ ākiyavai uḷḷaṉa. ki.mu. 1500kku muṉṉar cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kālam vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil vētamoḻi pēciyavarkaḷum tirāviṭa moḻi pēciyavarkaḷum neruṅkiya paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpu koṇṭu vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai itu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
The Earliest Indo - Aryan textï the compiled Rg Veda, shows severalinfluences of a non -Indo - Aryan, Dravidian element in the form ofphonetic changes, introduction of loan wordfs and names etc. Thesepresuppose the coexistence of the Vedic and Dravidian speaking peoplesin a cultural contact situation for a period, perhaps of centuries, beforethe compilation of the Rg Vedas (circa 1500 - 1300 BC)
- Bridget and Raymond Allchin (1988)
The rise of Civilisation in India nad pakistan.



 (v) intiyāvil vaḻaṅkum cāti muṟai
27. cilēṭṭar nūliṉ iyalkaḷ 5- 7 il tirāviṭamoḻi pēcunaruṭaiyē vaḻaṅkum cātimuṟai mutaliyavai paṟṟiya avaruṭaiya kaṇṇōṭṭam uḷḷatu. atil pala tamiḻiya moḻi pēci vanta tamiḻaka iṉakkuḻuviṉariṭam kūcainau ēṟkeṉavē nilaviya oru vakaiyāṉa (cāti pōṉṟa)iṉakkuḻut taṉmaikaḷai varuṇāciramak koḷkai vaḻipaṭṭa tāka virakākaintiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak [tamiḻiyak] kūṟukaḷ 
māṟṟi cātivēṟṟumaiyum koṭumaikaḷum atikarikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉparslēṭar. The Aryans found a system resembling caste, already in forceamong the Dravidian inhabitants and they adopted and modified it tosuit their own purposes.
28. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ 1923: cāti vēṟṟumaiyum pōliccaivarum; ē.el. paṣām1954,1955; kē.kē. piḷḷai 1977 Cohn 1987, Dirks 1987/1992; Inden 1986/ 1990 ākiyōr karuttum perumaḷavukku itutāṉ) (vi) 1924kku piṉṉar ittuṟaiyil āyvu vaḷarcci
29. intiyak kāviyaṅkaḷiṉ camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷil āriyamallāta(perumpālum tirāviṭa) kōṭpāṭukaḷē mikuntuḷḷamaiyai āntrē ep.sjōperku Andrea F. Sjoberg IJDL 38, (1999) pak 71-90 kaṭṭuraiyilNon Aryan (mainly Dravidian) features in the religious content of theIndian epics kaṭṭuraiyil kāṇka. avar Comparative Civilization Review 23: 1990 (pak 40-74) il intiya nākarika vaḷarccikku tirāviṭamoḻi pēcunar paṅkaḷippu maṟu āyvutēvai eṉṟa 1990 kaṭṭuraiyaiyum cilēṭṭar, nūlaippaṭippōr payilvatu nalam. anta 1990 kaṭṭuraiyiṉ tamiḻākkam maṭṭum cilēṭṭar nūl muṭintapiṉṉar iṇaippākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.


1. tirāviṭar
“tirāviṭar” eṉṟa peyar oru kurutiyiṉattaik (race)kuṟikkiṟatue ṉ ṟu koḷ vatai vi ṭa o ru moḻi yi ṉa t taik ku ṟi p patā ka kkoḷḷuvatē poruttamāṉatu. “tirāviṭa” eṉṟa camaskirutap(Sanskrit)peyarukkut *teṟku eṉṟa poruḷum uṇṭu.(tamiḻ eṉṟa coltāṉnāḷaṭaivil tamiḷ, tramiḷa, traviṭa, trāviṭa eṉat tirintatu eṉpatu iṉṟu(2014) moḻiyiyalāḷar aṉaivarum ēṟkum karuttu ākum) tirāviṭamoḻikaḷum perumaḷavukkut teṉ intiyāviṉ moḻikaḷākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. 1911-ām āṇṭaiya makkaṭ kaṇippiṉpaṭi6.20 kōṭi makkaḷ pēciya patiṉaintu moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷāka oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
2.tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ paṇpāṭṭil talaiciṟantatum tūymaimikkatum tamiḻē. itu intiyāvil 1.80 kōṭi makkaḷ pēcuvatu(1921)attuṭaṉ ilaṅkaiyil oru pakutiyiṉarum parmā, malēciyā mutaliya palapa ku ti ka ḷi l ku ṭiyē ṟi yu ḷ ḷa ta mi ḻa ru m u ṇ ṭu. ceṉṉai mutal kaṉṉiyākumarivarai atuvē tāymoḻiyāyuḷḷatu. teluṅku moḻiyai2.40kōṭi pēr ceṉṉaimutal kañcam māvaṭṭam varaippēcukiṉṟaṉar. kaṉṉaṭam tekkaṇap pakutiyil peruvaḻakkāṉatu; (maicūrttaṉiyaracilatuvē aracu moḻi.) 1.56 kōṭi makkaḷ pēcuvatu malaiyāḷam tiruvāṅkūr,kocci, malapār ākiya pakutikaḷukkuriyatu. atu kiṭṭattaṭṭa eḻupatu laṭcammakkaḷ pēcuvatu. ivaiyē talaiyāṉa tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ.
3. (i) teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam ākiyavai tamiḻaiviṭamikutiyākac camaskirutac cārpāṉa moḻikaḷiliruntu coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉpeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
(II) i n nā ṉ ku  mo ḻi ka ḷ nī ṅ ka lāṉa ma ṟ ṟa patiṉorutirāviṭamoḻikaḷum ilakkiyattuṟaic ciṟappu aṟṟavai.
4 . tirā vi ṭa moḻi ka ḷ  i n tiyā vi ṉ  te ṉ pa ku ti k ku ri ya ṉa eṉpataṟku mukkiyamāṉa oru vilakku “pirākuvi (Brahui)” moḻi; ataṉvāḻviṭam palucistāṉam; uṭaṟkūṟu, uḷappaṇpukaḷilum pirākuvipēcupavarkaḷ appakuti makkaḷiṭamiruntu vēṟu pāṭuṭaiyavarkaḷākavum, teṉintiya makkaḷuṭaṉ oppumaikaḷ pala uṭaiyavarkaḷākavum uḷḷaṉar.pirākuviyiṉ innilaimai kurutiyiṉattoṭarpiṉ (race) kāraṇamāka  irukkalām. eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
5. āṉāl, intiyāvil teṉ intiya tirāviṭarukkum,vaṭai ntiya makkaḷukkumiṭaiyē uṭaṟkūṟu cā rntaiṉavēṟupāṭ ṭu e l laikaḷai k kāṇaiyalā tu. kāṭu vāḻpaḻaṅkuṭiyiṉaṅkaḷai nīkkip pārttāl, mottattil teṉkiḻakkiliruntu
vaṭamēṟku nāṭic cellun tōṟum carācari uṭaṟkūṟṟuppaṭivam paṭippaṭiyāka ciṟuciṟu māṟutalaṭai vataiyē kāṇalām.
6. tirāviṭariṭaiyē (caṟṟē) taṉippaṭṭa nilaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ mīṉavarkuṭiyiṉar, kāṭuvāḻ kuṭiyiṉar cilarē, ivarkaḷil nākarikam kuṟaintaiṉattavarkaḷaic cilar tol tirāviṭar (Proto-Dravidians). eṉṟuvakukkiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cilar avarkaḷait ‘tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭavar’ (Pre-Dravidians);  tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉ ūṭāṭi avarkaḷ moḻikaḷaik kaikkoṇṭavar eṉṟum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar, ituvē mutal karuttaiviṭap
poruttamāṉatu eṉalām.
7. tirāviṭamoḻi pēcum makkaḷil (muṉ pattiyiṟcuṭṭikkāṭṭiya)irucārārait tavira maṟṟavarkaḷiṭaiyē mika neruṅkiya kurutiyiṉattoṭarpāṉa oṟṟumai racial affinity kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ‘tirāviṭaiṉam’ eṉṟa toṭarai vaḻaṅkamuṭivatu itaṉālēyē. attoṭar iṉṟumtirāviṭamoḻi pēcivarum makkaḷaimaṭṭumiṉṟi, āriyamoḻi pēcunarintiyāvukku vanta piṉṉar avarkaḷ tākkattāl camaskirutac cāyalkoṇṭa moḻikaḷai iṉṟu pēcum piṟa makkaḷaiyum cuṭṭuvatākum.
8. pirākuvi nīṅkalāka ēṉaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaic camaskirutaccārpāṉa moḻikaḷ intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭi nōkki nerukkittaḷḷiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatiliruntu,“camaskirutam pēciya āriyap paṭaiyeṭuppāḷarkaḷ ēṉaiya intiyap pakutikaḷiliruntu avarkaḷai muṟṟilumaḻittoḻittuviṭṭārkaḷ, turattiviṭṭārkaḷ” eṉṟu koḷvatu tavaṟu.pirañcu moḻi ilattīṉac cārpāṉa moḻi eṉpatiliruntu “juliyascīsariṉ urōmappaṭaikaḷ keltiya moḻi pēciya ‘kāl’ Gaul (iṉṟaiyapirāṉ°) makkaḷai aḻittoḻittuviṭṭaṉar!” eṉṟu koḷḷa muṭiyātu.atu pōṉṟatē itum.
9(I) ittakaiya cūḻalkaḷil  irumoḻikaḷukkiṭaiyē ēṟpaṭum pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷil veṟṟitōlvikaḷiṉ muṭivu ōraḷavu cuṟṟuccārpukaḷaiyum, ōraḷavu moḻiyiṉ paṇpu kaḷaim poṟuttatu ākum. ‘kāl’nāṭṭililattīṉ moḻikku oru peruṅkuṟaipāṭu iruntatu, ataip pēciyavar oru ciṉṉañciṟu kuḻuviṉar; āyiṉum muṭivil eñci niṉṟatu atuvē!itaṟku utaviya kāraṇaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu; oṉṟu atu veṟṟikaṇṭavar moḻieṉpatu; maṟṟoṉṟu, kēylik, piriṭṭāṉiya moḻikaḷaiviṭa lattīṉ moḻiayalār kaṟṟu mēṟkoḷḷac ciṟitu eḷitāyiruntatu eṉpatu.
(II) intiyāviṉ mikappala pakutikaḷilum vaṭamoḻic cārpāṉamoḻi kaḷuk kum tirāviṭamo ḻikaḷukku mi ṭaiyē naṭan ta pōrāṭṭamperitum pirāṉcu nāṭṭup paṇṭaiya moḻiyiṉap pōrāṭṭattuṭaṉ mikaneruṅkiya oppumai uṭaiyatākavē iruntirukka vēṇṭum.
10. tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ yāvumē kaṟpataṟku mikavum kaṭumaiyuṭaiyavai. vaṭamoḻic cārpāṉa moḻikaḷaiviṭa avai kaṭumaiyāṉavai eṉpatu evvaḷavu tolaivu uṇmai eṉṟu iṉṟum kāṇalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ nāṭṭu moḻikaḷāyirukkumiṭaṅkaḷil airōppiya atikāri kaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ intiyap paṇiyāṭkaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa toṭarpu moḻi eppōtum āṅkilamākavē irukkiṟatu. ēṉaiya intiyap pakutikaḷilō atu eppōtum intustāṉiyākattāṉ irukkiṟatu. ceṉṉaiyilpakkiṅkām karnāṭṭik ālaikaḷai airōppiya mēlāṇmai niṟuviyapōtu,intiya moḻipeyarppāḷarkaḷai vaittuc ceyalpaṭavillai. māṟṟu muṟaioṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu .toḻilāḷar piḷḷaikaḷukkum pakuti nērat toḻilāḷarkaḷukkum paḷḷikaḷ vaittu āṅkilaṅ kaṟṟukkoṭukkummuṟaiyai mika eḷitākap piṉpaṟṟi veṟṟipeṟa muṭintatu. itiliruntutirāviṭar perumpāṉmaik kuṭimakkaḷāy, avarkaḷiṭaiyē ciṟukuḻuviṉarāṉa āriyap paṭaiyeṭuppāḷarkaḷ āṭciyiṉattavarkaḷāka amaivuṟum iṭaṅkaḷilellām, āḷum iṉattavar āḷap paṭumiṉattavarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu maṟaintu viṭṭapōtiluṅkūṭa, āriyaccārpāṉa āṭci yāḷar moḻiyē nāṭṭu moḻiyākiviṭukiṟatu eṉṟukāṇalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum vaṭamoḻi cārnta moḻikaḷum uṟavāṭumiṭaṅkaḷilellām tirāviṭamoḻi aḻivuṟṟu, vaṭamoḻi cārnta moḻiveṟṟi peṟṟōṅkum marapu iṉṟum toṭarntu nikaḻkiṟatu.
11. kurutiyiṉap paraval (racial spread)cāṉṟukaḷāl intiyāviṉiṉaākkattil tirāviṭa iṉakkūṟē maṟṟellā iṉakkūṟukaḷaiyumviñcik kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mottattil iṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ paravalkurutiyiṉap paraval iraṇṭum oṉṟupōlavē uḷḷaṉa. itumaṭṭumaṉṟu;vēṟu oru ceytiyum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. camaskirutattiṉ olippumuṟaiyāṉatu, tamiḻpōṉṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, maṟṟa intō – jermāṉiya(āriyaiṉa) moḻikaḷukkum iṭaippaṭṭa nilaiyilēyē amaintirukkiṟatu. iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa vaṭivil irukkuvētam uruvāṉa kālattilēyē camaskirutamoḻi pēciya makkaḷil oru kaṇicamāṉapakutiyiṉar tirāviṭa iṉattavarākavē iruntaṉar.
12. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ittirāviṭar yār? intiyāvukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa makkaṭ pakutiyiṉaruṭaṉ avarkaḷ iṉamuṟait toṭarpukaḷettakaiyavai? avarkaḷ intiyāvukku evvāṟu vantaṉar? ‘naṭunilakkaṭaliṉam Mediterrranean race’. eṉpatai akaṉṟa poruḷil, (avviṉattoṭarpuṭaiya aṉaittaiyum kuṟippatākak) koṇṭāl, tirāviṭamakkaḷiṉ talaimai iṉakkūṟu naṭunilakkaṭal iṉattiṉ oru kiḷaiyēeṉalām. potuvāka nōkkiṉāl talaiyōṭṭuppaṭivam, mayiramaitippaṇpu niṟam, kaṇniṟam, uṟuppamaiti, uṭalamaitikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil avviṉattavaruṭaṉ tirāviṭar kuṟippiṭattakka oṟṟumai uṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉalām.
13. iru cārariṭaiyēyum mikat teḷivākak kuṟippiṭattakka vēṟupāṭuoṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ. atuvē mēṉiniṟam. potuvākat tirāviṭar niṟammaṟṟavaraiviṭak kaṟuppuccāyal mikkatu. āṉāl, carācari ittāliyar allatu cappāṉiyariṉ cempoṉmēṉi niṟattiliruntu nīkirōviṉ kaṟuppu niṟamvaraiyuḷḷa ellā vakai niṟaṅkaḷum avarkaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷaṉa. mayirniṟam,mukattōṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟil vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa; orumuṉaiyil pampitta curuṭṭai mayiraiyum maṟumuṉaiyil meṉmaiyaiyumkāṇalām. naṭunilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷaiviṭat tiṇṇamāṉa utaṭukaḷumcappaiyāṉa mūkkukaḷum avarkaḷil cilariṭam uḷḷaṉa.
14. ivvēṟupāṭukaḷ yāvum, tirāviṭar intiyāvukkuḷ vantapiṉ,avarkaḷukku muṉṉarē iṅku vāḻnta kaṟuppu mēṉi, tiṇṇiya utaṭukaḷaiuṭaiya muṉṉaip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuṭaṉ iṉakkalappup peṟṟataṉālēṟpaṭṭavaiyē eṉa eṉakkut tōṟṟukiṟatu. tirāviṭar ikkalappiṉālmēṉiniṟam māṟi veppamaṇṭalattil piḻaittu vāḻum āṟṟalaippeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. immāṟutalāl avarkaḷ aḻakamaiti oru ciṟitukuṟaintuḷḷatu; ciṟappākat tamiḻakattil tāṉ itu ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu.
15. ‘āṅkilō intiyar’ eṉṟu muṉpu aḻaikkappaṭṭa intiya vāḻairōppi yariṭaiyē ceṉṉaiyil vaḻaṅkiya paḻamoḻi. “tīviṉaiyiṉ maruṭcivalaiyiliruntu emmaik kāppāṟṟuvīrāka’ eṉṟa vēṇṭukōḷukku iṇaṅkik rttar ulakukku ttamiḻaka naṅkaiyai  aṉuppiṉār !”eṉpatākum. tirāviṭa makkaḷil teṉkōṭiyiluḷḷa tamiḻp peṇṭiraippotuvākak kuṟippatil itu caṟṟu mikaippaṭuttiyatākum! teluṅkar,kaṉṉaṭiyar, malaiyāḷikaḷ ākiyavaraip pārkkilum tamiḻar karumaiccāyal kūṭiyavarāyum, mēṉivaṉappukkuṟaintavarākavumē uḷḷaṉar. potuvākatteṟkē celluntōṟum vēḷāṇmai ceyyum makkaḷiṭaiyēyum, ciṟappāka‘ātitirāviṭar’ eṉṟu ceṉṉai aracāṅkam kuṟippiṭum makkaḷiṭaiyēyum, tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa Pre Dravidian iṉamakkaḷiṉ cāyal mikutiyākavēirukkiṟatu.
16(I) tirāviṭariṉ niṟa māṟupāṭṭukkuk kāraṇamāṉa paṇpukkūṟukaḷ iraṇṭu vakaippaṭṭavai. mikapperumpālāṉa potumakkaḷkaṭuveyilil iruntu uḻaikka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. attuṭaṉaraiyil iraṇṭu muḻat tuṇiyum talaikkuṭṭaiyaiyum tavira vēṟupātukāppu illai. eṉavē tōliṉ karuniṟa aṇukkaḷ nalla pātukāppākaamaikiṉṟaṉa. innilaiyil iyaṟkaiyiṉ tēr vu muṟaip pa ṭi e llāintiyaraiyum pōlavē tirāviṭarum veṇpoṉṉiṟattaiyē peritumpārāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar!
(II) nāṭakamēṭaikaḷil peṇkaḷāka naṭikkum ciṟuvarkaḷ mukattilveṇpoṭi pūci atai veṇpoṉniṟamākka arumpāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar.airōppiyar pārvaiyil atu muḻu veṟṟi peṟavillaieṉpatu vēṟu! maṇappeṇkaḷ vakaiyil cenniṟattiṟkēṟpa matippu uyarkiṟatu. maṇa viḷamraṅkaḷil māppiḷḷai vīṭṭār, peṇvīṭṭār ākiya iru cārārumē
niṟattai vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar.
(III) mēlni lai k ku ṭumpa ṅ ka ḷi l māppiḷḷaiyai iṇaṅ ka cceyvataṟkāka māmaṉār tarum cītaṉam peṇkaḷ cenniṟamāyirukkumaḷavukkuk kuṟaivu. itaṉāl cenniṟam, cātiyaik kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻiccollē mutalil niṟattaik kuṟikkiṟatu. cātimuṟai uyarvu tāḻvum ōraḷavu cenniṟam, karuniṟam eṉṟavāṟē amaintuḷḷatu.
17. āka tirāviṭar naṭunilakkaṭaṟpakutip pēriṉattiṉ oru kiḷaiyiṉar;piṟa iṉakkurutikkalappāl ciṟitu māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉar eṉpatai ōraḷavukkuēṟṟuk koṇṭāl, avarkaḷ tam mūlamutaliṭattiliruntu tolpaḻaṅkālattilēyē intiyā vukku vantuviṭṭaṉareṉalām. naṭukkaṭal iṉamMediterranean race kiḻakkāp pirikkāviliruntu vantirukkalām; avarkaḷil oru pakutiyiṉar araipiyā, teṉ pāracikam vaḻiyāka intiyāvukku vantaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷalām eṉpar ji. eliyaṭ cumit.
18. ikkōṭpāṭṭukku etirk kōṭpāṭu, naṭunilakkaṭaliṉattiṉ mūlamutaliṭamē intiyāviltāṉ eṉpatē. iṉṟaiya cintukaṅkaic camaveḷi paṇṭu kaṭalāyirunta paḻamai ūḻiyil teṉ intiyāāpirikkāvuṭaṉ iṇaintiruntateṉṟum, mūlat tirāviṭakkuṭiyakamatuveṉṟum, icceyti tamiḻ marapuraikaḷil toṉikkiṟa teṉṟum kāṭṭamuyalvatu poruttamaṟṟatu, ēṟṟukkoḷḷa iyalātatu.(kaṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉṟaiya nilaiyai orukōṭi āṇṭukku maṉṉarē aṭaintuviṭṭaṉa! appoḻutu maṉitakkuraṅku iṉam kūṭa uruvākavillai!!)
19. ekiptil naṭunilakkaṭaliṉam mikapperum paḻamai uṭaiyatueṉpatum, ‘mēlīṭākap pārkkum aḷavilēyē tirāviṭa iṉappaṇpukkum ataṉcūḻalukkum irukkum poruttattaiviṭa, naṭunilakkaṭaliṉattiṉ paṇpuṭaṉataṉ cūḻal peritum poruntiyirukkiṟatu eṉpatum cariyāṉa muṭipu etue ṉ patait ti ṇ ṇamākak kāṭṭuki ṉ ṟa ṉa . ceṉṉaiyi li ru n tuiṅkilāntukku varum māṇavar ak kuḷir nāṭṭu nilaimaiyuṭaṉ eḷitākaicaintu viṭukiṟār; māṟākac ceṉṉaikku vantatum ippakuti veppanilaiyil nāṉ iṭarppaṭṭēṉ.
20. tirāviṭar veḷiyiliruntu vantārkaḷāṉāl, enta vaḻiyākavantirukkalām? “muṟkālap paṇpāṭukaḷiṉ iṭappeyarccikaḷ” eṉṟanūlil (pakkam:80) pērāciriyar kirāḵvṭaṉ eliyaṭ cumit “putiyapaṇpāṭṭukkuriya makkaḷ atāvatu mēlticaiyiliruntu vanta kaṭalvaḻip payaṇikaḷ tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷuṭaṉ kurutikkalappuṟṟu ataṉ payaṉākat tirāviṭarāyiṉar”eṉkiṟār. avarkaḷ ki.mu.3000 mutaṟkoṇṭu, potuvākavum; ciṟappāka, ki.mu. 800-ai aṭuttupperuvāriyākavum, puṟappaṭṭu, ekiptil uruvāki vaḷarnta ñāyiṟṟuk kal(heliolithic)vaḻipāṭṭup paṇpāṭṭuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, piṟa iṭap paṇpāṭukaḷaiyum
kalantu koṇṭavarāy, paḻaiya ulaku putiya ulaku iraṇṭiṉ kaṭaltīraṅkaḷilum parantaṉar.
(II) avar kūṟaṟu ‘mutatuccempaṭavarummīṉ cempaṭavarum ulōkavāṇarumāṉa immēṉāṭṭuk kaṭalōṭikaḷ, pallāyiram mail kaṭal payaṇam vaḻip parantaṉar’ eṉpatākum. eṉavē appaṭik kuṭiyēṟiyavaruḷ āṭavar palarāka iruntirukka muṭiyātu; peṇṭir cilarkūṭa iruttalaritu. avar kuṟippiṭum appaḻaṅ kālattil, ciṟanta tirāviṭap paṇpuuruvāvataṟkup pōtiya kāla iṭaiveḷi iruntirukka vaḻiyillai; āriyappaṭaiyeṭuppaimikap pintiyatākak koṇṭālum ataṟkum muṉṉarēmēṉāṭṭu kīḻnāṭṭup paṇṭai marapukaḷ kalantu putumarapu oruvāki,vaḷarntirukka muṭiyātu. ki.mu. 1000 - 800 kālakaṭṭattukku muṉṉarētirāviṭar intiyāvukkuḷ vantirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu.
21. tiru. ṭapḷyū.jē.peri (1923: The children of the sun  ‘katiravaṉ  cēykaḷ’ eṉṟa nūlil)ekiptiya nākarikattiṉar āṟāvatu ekiptiya aracakula  marapukkālam 3 (ki.mu.2500)mutal intiyak kaṭaṟkaraikaḷuṭaṉ pōkkuvaravut  toṭarpu koṇṭi runtaṉar eṉpataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ tarukiṟār. “paṇṭaik  kaṭalōṭikaḷ” (1917) eṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyil eliyaṭ cumit itē muṟpaṭṭa  kālakkuṟippai ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷār. ipputukkaruttu poruttamikkatē.  āṉāl ataṉaiyum oru ciṟu māṟutaluṭaṉ tāṉ eṉṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷa  muṭikiṟatu. (cintu nākarikam ki.mu.3000kkum mika muṟpaṭṭa teṉpatu  1924kkuppiṉṉar akaḻvāyvil teriyavantuḷḷatu.)  
22. kaṭalvaḻip payaṇakkōṭpāṭṭai maṟuttāl āpkāṉistāṉ,  palucistāṉ nilavaḻiyai ēṟkanērum. paluccistāṉ kiḻakkellaiyai  aṭuttut taṟpōtu pirākuvi iṉam amaintiruppatai maṉatiṟkoṇṭāl  (kaipar kaṇavāy vaḻi nuḻaiyum) āpkāṉistāṉ vaḻi poruntātu.  iṉṉoru pātai mecapoṭṭēmiyāvaiyum intiyāvaiyum iṇaikkum  irayilpōkkuvarattukkāka1920 kaḷil ārāyntu kaiviṭappaṭṭa paḻampātai  ākum. atu pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāviliruntu vaṭakiḻakkākak kuveṭṭā vantu  piṉ teṟku nōkkip  pōlaṉ kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkuḷ  nuḻaikiṟatu. ituvē avarkaḷ vanta pātaiyāyirukkalām.

23(I) mecapoṭṭēmiyāvukkum intiyāvukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa pakuti  vaṟaṭci yaṭaintatu iṭaikkālattilē tāṉ eṉ ṟu namakku t teriyavaruki ṟa tu . tirāviṭar iṅku va n ta kāla t ti l vēṭar , mīṉavar kuḻu  viṉarākavē iruntaṉar eṉalām. attakaiyavar toṭarntu muṉṉēṟat  tūṇṭuvataṟkup pōtumāṉa  nīrvaḷam, ceṭiyiṉavaḷam, vēṭṭai uyiriṉavaḷam ākiyavai ivviṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷalām.  koṇṭāl, tirāviṭariṉ mutaṟpuṭai peyarcci (migration) piṉīciyariṉ  mutaṟ puṭaipeyarcciyaip peritum ottatākiṟatu. ēṉeṉil piṉīciyar  marapukkataikaḷiṉpaṭiyē, avarkaḷ pāracika vaḷaikuṭāp pakutiyiliruntu  puṟappaṭṭu arēpiyak kaṭaṟkarai vaḻiyākac ceṅkaṭaṟpakuticeṉṟu, ataṉ  piṉṉarē nilaiyākap pālastīṉak kaṭaṟkaraip pakutiyil taṅkiṉar eṉṟu  aṟikiṟōm.
(II). akkālattil arēpiyā piṟa makkaḷait taṉpakkamākak īrttu  uḷnāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟṟuvikkum nilaiyil illai eṉalām. āṉāl  intiyāviṉ mēlpuṟak karaiyil cintu āṟum, kāmpē vaḷaikuṭāvil  toṭaṅkip pālakkāṭṭuk kaṇavāyvarai (iṉṟu irayil pātai cellum  pōkkil) ceṉṟa paḻaiyapātaiyum iruntaṉa. nilaviyal aṭippaṭaiyil  vaḷamāṉa intiyac camaveḷikaḷai nōkki makkaḷai īrkka, ivvāyppukaḷ  pōtiyavai.
24.(a). tirāviṭar vanta kālavaraiyaṟaipaṟṟi iṉi ārāyvōm.  tirāviṭa nākarikam intiyāvilēyē uruvāṉatu. veḷiccūḻalkaḷiṉ  taṭaṅka ḷ atil cila iruntālum , atu pe rumpālum intiyac  cūḻa liṭaiyē uruvāṉatē e ṉ pa tu kīḻē vi ḷa k ka p paṭu m . eṉavē  ivvakaiyil kīḻvarum ceytikaḷai nām karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭum:
1 . na ṭu ni la k ka ṭa l veḷi yi li ru n tu pa lu c ci stāṉ vaḻiyāka  intiyāvukku varum pātai kaṭṭāyamāka mecapoṭṭēmiyā vaḻic  ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum.
2. mēlē viḷakkiya cūḻnilaikaḷai nōkka ippuṭaipeyarcci  cumēriya nākarikat tōṟṟattukku muṟpaṭṭatāyirukka vēṇṭum.
(ā) tirāviṭar pāpilōṉiyā vaḻiyākavē vantum, intiyāvukku  vēḷāṇmaip paṇpāṭṭaik koṇṭu vantirukkavillai eṉṟāl,  pāpilōṉiyāvil vēḷāṇmaip paṇpāṭu piṟappataṟkumuṉṉar  avarkaḷ avvaḻiyāka vantirukka vēṇṭum. (māṟāka avarkaḷ  avvaṟivait tammuṭaṉ koṇṭuvantaṉar eṉṟāl, uṇmai itaṟku  nērmāṟāyiruntirukka vēṇṭum.)
(i) intap puṭaipeyarcci vakaiyilum, itupōṉṟa vēṭṭuva  vāḻkkaip paṭinilaiyil uḷḷa piṟa puṭaipeyarccikaḷ vakaiyilum pōtiya  tiṭṭamāṉa vivaraṅkaḷ terivatillai; kāraṇaṅkaḷai uṉṉikkattāṉ  iyalum. ivvāḻkkaip paṭittaratti luḷḷavarkaḷ puṭaipeyarvatu eḷitē; āṉālum ētēṉum valimaivāynta tūṇṭu talillāmal ittakaiya  puṭaipeyarcci naṭantirukkum eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu.
25. taṭpaveppa māṟutalkaḷai nīkkip pārttāl, ‘vēṭṭaik  karuvikaḷil mukkiyamāṉa karuvi mēmpāṭum’ tūṇṭutalukkuk kāraṇamā  yiruntirukkalām. vaḷaitaṭi, īṭṭi tavira vēṟu karuvikaḷ illāta oru  nilappakutiyil, oru iṭattil vil, ampu kaṇṭu  piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu  vaittukkoḷvōm. mu ta li l av viṭa t ti l uṇavu mikutiyākak  kiṭaikkum; iṟappiṉaiviṭap piṟappu mikutiyākum; āṉāl nāḷaṭaivil  vēṭṭai  vila ṅ ku ka ḷi l eṇṇikkai kuṟaintu putiya vēṭṭai nilaṅ  kaḷai nāṭip putuk karuvikaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittavarkaḷ etticaiyilāvatu iṭam  peyara nēriṭum. māṉaikkollum karuviyāl maṉitaṉaiyum kollalām.  ākavē putukkaruvikkārar puṭaipeyarcciyai (migration)piṟar taṭukka  muṭiyātu. akkaruviyaik kaikkoṇṭa varō eṉil, uṭaṉ kalantu tāmum  puṭaipeyaravē eṇṇuvar.
	26. vēḷāṇmaikku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil airōppāvil uḷḷa  iṉaṅkaḷ (races), paṇpāṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ puṭaipeyarccipaṟṟik  kiṭaittuḷḷa pala takavalkaḷai oruṅku iṇaittup pārttāl mēṟ  koṇṭa karutukōḷ cariyākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. ayalār puṭai  peyarcci (migration) illāmalum paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ paraviyuḷḷaṉa  puṭaipeyarcciyuṭaṉ iṇaintum palakūṟukaḷ paraviyuḷḷaṉa. iṇaintu  naṭaipeṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷilellām putuvaraviṉar ‘vēṭṭaiyil payaṉpaṭum  ētāvatu putuk karuvikaḷai’ u ṭaṉ koṇn ṭa va ntu ḷ ḷaṉa r. eṭu ttu k kāṭṭaka  na ṭu ni la k ka ṭa l i ṉa t ta va ru m a si li ya p (Az i l i a n)  paṇpāṭṭiṉarum vaṭakku nōkkip paravukai yil vil ampukaḷaik koṇṭu  ceṉṟaṉar. mēṟku nōkkic ceṉṟa māklemōciyap (Maglemosian)  paṇpāṭṭiṉar vēṭṭai nāykaḷait tammuṭaṉ koṇṭuceṉṟaṉar. āka  tirāviṭar intiyāviṟkuḷ varuvataṟku vēṭṭaiyiṉ vakaituṟaik  karuvikaḷil nikaḻnta mēmpāṭu kāraṇamāyiruntirukka vēṇṭum  eṉpatu ēṟkattakkatu.
27.(I)iṉṟirukkum moḻikaḷiliruntē paṇṭait tirāviṭa  vēṭuvarkaḷiṉ maṉanilai paṟṟiya nuṇṇiya (āṉāl muḻuviḷakkamaṟṟa) oḷi  kiṭṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil tamiḻē tūymaimikkatātalāl, ataṉ  utaviyai nāṭuvōm. tamiḻ mika akalavirivuṭaiya nākarikattiṉ nīṇṭakāla  vaḷarcciyil uruvāṉatu. āyiṉum annākarikattait tōṟṟuvittu    ammoḻiyai cemmaip paṭuttiya maṉappaṇpu avarkaḷ vēṭṭuva  mūtātaiyariṭattiliruntē vantirukka vēṇṭum. (orukkāl ammūtātai  yariṭam appaṇpu mutirānilaiyil iruntirukkalām.)  

(II) tamiḻmoḻi ataṉ nayanuṭpam, aṟivut tiṭpam mutaviya  vaṟṟil mī ppe ru m taṉic ci ṟa p pu ṭai ya tu . a taṉaic colō ṭṭu  nilai moḻi(Agglutinative language )eṉpar. āyiṉum coltiripu  moḻiyiṉ toṭakka nilaiyil uḷḷateṉṟē (Initial Inflexional stage)  ataik kuṟippatu cari. atil viṉaikaḷukkut tiṇai, pāl, iṭa, vikutikaḷ  uṇṭu. āṉāl avai avvav iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷē; paṭarkkai  orumaikkuriya āṇpāl, peṇpāl, aḵṟiṇaivaṭivaṅkaḷ avaṉ, avaḷ,  atu eṉpavai; ellāk kālaṅkaḷukkum uriya itē iṭaṅkaḷiṉ viṉai  vikutikaḷ āṉ, āḷ, atu eṉpavaiyē.
	(II) āṉāl peyarkaḷukkum, iṭappeyarkaḷukkum vikutikaḷ uṇṭu.  ivaiyum perumpālāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil taṉiccoṟkaḷ eṉṟu teḷivākat  teriyum. perumpālum ellāp peyarccoṟkaḷum orē mātiriyākat  tiripaṭaikiṉṟaṉa. vēṟṟumai vikutikaḷum orumai paṉmai iraṇṭilum  māṟupāṭaṟṟavai. paṉmaikkup paṉmai vikutiyai mutalil cērttup piṉṉar  vēṟṟumaiyuṭaṉ u ru pu pe ṟu vatē a ta ṟ ku ri ya ci ṟa p pu . ( mā ṭ ṭu k ku ;  māṭukaḷukku)
	(III) etirmaṟai viṉaiyākkattil tirāviṭac cintaṉai  nuṭpam naṉku viḷaṅkum. nikaḻkāla viṉaikku nikaḻkālac ciṉṉamākiya  nikaḻkāla iṭainilai vēṇṭum. atupōlavē iṟantakālattukkum, etir  kālattukkum avvak kālattukkuriya iṭainilaikaḷ vēṇṭum. ittakaiya  (kālam kāṭṭum) iṭaikaḷiṉ iṇaippu illāmal pakutiyuṭaṉ nēraṭiyāka  iṭavikuti iṇaikkappaṭṭāl, viṉai yiṉ ceyal iṟappu, nikaḻvu,  etirvu ākiya mukkālattilum nikaḻavillai eṉṟu maṟukkappaṭukiṟatu  - atāvatu atu etirmaṟai (negative) ākiviṭukiṟutu. ceytal eṉṉum  poruḷuṭaiya cey eṉṟa viṉaippakutiyai eṭuttuk koṇṭāl.  nikaḻkālam: (cey-kiṟ-ēṉ) ceykiṟēṉ.  iṟantakālam: (cey-t-ēṉ) ceytēṉ.  etirkālam: (cey-v-ēṉ) ceyvēṉ.āṉāl etirmaṟai; (cey-ēṉ) ceyyēṉ. (mikaiyāṉa yakaram  puṇariyal muṟaiyil vēṇṭiya uṭampaṭumey maṭṭumē.)
	iṅkē ‘ceyyēṉ’ eṉṟa etirmaṟaiyiṉ muḻupporuḷ ceykiṟē  ṉillai, ceytēṉillai, ceyvēṉillai eṉpatē. itu aṟivu nuṭpamum  ti ṟamu m vāynta muṟai. āyiṉum e ti r maṟaiyi ṉ ellā nuṇṇayaṅ  kaḷaiyum iṅku viḷakkavillai. tamiḻar maṟṟa ellā moḻikaḷilum nam  āṅkilattilum iruppatu pōṉṟē, “illai” eṉṟa collaiyum  koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. itil piṟamoḻikaḷaip pōlavē iṭam maṭṭumiṉṟik
kālamum kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu. (iḵtaṉṟik kālamkuṟittu, iṭam kuṟikkāta  maṟṟoru etirmaṟaiyum, kālam iṭam iraṇṭum kuṟikkāta maṟṟōr  etirmaṟaiyum uḷḷaṉa.)
	(IV) viṉāvā cakaṅkaḷilum itai nilaiyaik kāṇkiṟōm .  vācakattil entac colluṭaṉum cērkkakkūṭiya iṭainilaiyākiya “ā”  oru viṉākkuṟiyīṭu. atu ilattīṉ moḻiyiluḷḷa “ne” eṉṟa acai  pōṉṟatu. aiyakkuṟippuṭaṉ itēpōṉṟu ellāc coṟkaḷuṭaṉum  cērkkakkūṭiya maṟṟoru viṉāk kuṟi “ō” eṉpatu. itu ilattīṉ  moḻiyiṉ “tum” aṭaiyai ottatu. ilattīṉattiluḷḷa “noṉṉe”  (=illaiyā!) eṉṟa acaiyiṉ poruḷ vēṇṭumāṉāl, ‘illai’ eṉṟa  collai acaikaḷuṭaṉ cērkkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl iccolluṭaṉ “ā”  “ō” eṉṟa acaikaḷai nēraṭiyākac cērkkāmal, viṉākkuṟippu ēṟkum  aṭippaṭaic colluṭaṉ cērkkiṉṟaṉar. curuṅkakkūṟiṉāl, tamiḻ  moḻiyiṉ viṉā ākkamuṟai māntaṉ cintaṉai nayattaic  ciṟappākak kāṭṭukiṟatu.
 	(V) immoḻiyiṉ maṟṟoru taṉicciṟappu uyarvu vaḻakkukaḷ  mikappala  eṉpatu  ākum . uyarvup paṉmai muṉṉilaiyil maṭṭu miṉṟip  paṭarkkaiyilum iṭam peṟukiṟatu. itu ciṟappāka, vallāṇmaiyuṭaiya  maṉṉar, marapumuṟai vaḻuvāta aracavaiyōr, camayakuravar ākiyavarkaḷ iṭam  peṟṟirunta piṟkāla camūka amaippu muṟaiyiṉ viḷaivē eṉṉalām.
	(VI) tamiḻ yāppumuṟai kirēkka ilattīṉa moḻikaḷaip pōlavē  aḷavu (quality)aṭippaṭaiyāṉatu. atil etukai uṇṭu; āṉāl  ivvetukai aṭiyīṟṟi lalla, aṭiyiṉ mutalil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. mōṉai  uṇṭu; āṉāl itu yāppaṭikaḷai iṇaippataṉṟu, aṭiyiṉuḷ iyalvatu.  28. tamiḻicai kāṟcuraṅkaḷ (quarter tones )aṭippaṭaiyāṉatu. atāvatu  icainilaiyiluḷḷa ēḻu curaṅkaḷiṉ iṭattil tamiḻicaiyil irupatteṭṭuc curaṅkaḷ uṇṭu.
	29. nuṭpa ārāyccittiṟamum muḻuniṟai viḷakkap paṇpum intiyap  paṇpāṭṭiṉ taṉicciṟappup paṇpukaḷ. ivai ippaṇpukaḷai iyaṟṟavum  iyakkavum valla ciṟanta makkaḷiṉattiṉiṭamiruntē tōṟṟiyirukka  vēṇṭum. attakaiya āṟṟal tamiḻ moḻi vaḷarcciyilum iṭam peṟṟatu  iyalpē. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil tūymaimikka tamiḻ icciṟappukaḷai ucca  aḷavil koṇṭilaṅkukiṟatu; vēṟu enta intiya moḻiyaiyum viṭa tamiḻil  ippaṇpu mikuti.
pērāciriyar hec.jē. ḵvḷūriṉ (ḻa.tu.kuṭanaracana) kuṟippu  teṉṉintiya makkaḷiṉ iṉappirivukaḷum (cayaunaḷa) avaṟṟiṭaiyē paṇṭu irunta  paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpukaḷum:
	30.teṉṉintiya makkaḷiṭaiyē talai(maṇṭaiyōṭṭu)vaṭivamaiti  (craniology) palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl teṉṉintiyat teṉ  pātiyil atu nīṇṭu oṭuṅkic (dolicocephaly)caṟṟu uyarntuḷḷatu. oru  cila taṉiyiṭaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka, tirāviṭa intiyāvil talaikaḷ potuvāka  nīḷamākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. mēṉi maṭṭum poṉṉiṟamāy iruppiṉum  maṟṟappaṭi ittuṭaṉ otta maṇṭaiyōṭṭumātirikaḷ (types) teṉmēṟku  āciyāvil āṅkāṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē (niṟam, mūkkup  parimāṇam tavira maṟṟappaṭi) arēpiyāviluḷḷa cemittiyat talai, tirāviṭat  talai paṭiva oṟṟumaipaṟṟi aiyappaṭa vēṇṭiyatillai. itupōlavē vaṭa  āppirikkā maṟṟum naṭunilakkaṭalil paranta mēlaip pakuti cūḻnta nāṭu kaḷiṉ  makkaḷiṉ carācari parimāṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉum oppuṭaiyatu.
31. mika nīṇṭoṭuṅkiya uyartalaikaḷ, kuṭṭaiyāṉa akaṉṟa  mūkkukaḷ, muṉaippāṉa (palar tiṇṇiya utaṭukaḷuṭaṉ) mōvāykaḷ  ākiyavaṟṟai uṭaiya taṉi naparkaḷai nām mēṟkūṟiya ellā iṉak  kuḻuviṉariṭaiyilumē(races) pārkkiṟōm. ippaṇpukaḷ mūlam avarkaḷ  pirāṉciluḷḷa ‘kirimālṭi’k kukaiyiliruntum (Grimaldi caves) ‘kōmpē  ka p pe’ li li ru n tu m (Comb e c a pe l l e) a kaḻā y vi l kaṇṭeṭutta  paḻaṅkālap paṭiva mātirikaḷiṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ivarkaḷaip pōṉṟa paṇṭaip paṭivaṅkaḷiṉ paṭiva vaḷarcci mutircciyiṉ  viḷaivākavē, aipīriyat tīvak kuṟaiyiliruntu (speyṉ, pōrccukal)  teṉṉāṭu varai taṟkālam talaimai vāyntuḷḷa iṉaṅkaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa;  paṟpala cūḻal tākkutalkaḷāl avarkaḷ vaḷarcci yaṭainta muṟai maṭṭum ōraḷavu vēṟu paṭṭirukkalām.
32. teṉmēṟku āciyāvil kuḷirkālam nīṇṭatu. ākavē  mūccukkāṟṟu mūccuppaikkuḷ cellum vaḻiyil ataṉai vetuvetup  pākkuvataṟkēṟṟa muṟaiyil nīṇṭa mūkku amaintuḷḷatu. itaṉāl  mukattiṉ potu muṉaippum mikuntuḷḷatu. vaṭaāppirikkāvilum teṉ  intiyāvilum veppamaṇṭalattiṉ taṭpaveppa nilai mēṉiyiṉ karu  niṟakkūṟukaḷaip pēraḷavu perukkiyuḷḷatu. attuṭaṉ tōliṉ mayirt  toḷaikaḷ taḷarcciyaṭaivatāl mayirpala iṭaṅkaḷil curuṭṭaiyākum  pōkkum uṇṭu. āyiṉum immāṟṟaṅkaḷ āppirikkāvil hāmittiya  iṉattār vāḻum vaṭaāppirikkap pakutikku teṟkēyuḷḷa akalcamaveḷi  yiltāṉ muḻuniṟaivu peṟamuṭintuḷḷatu. āṉāl teṉ intiyāvil  teṟku nōkki varuntōṟum atu aruki aruki vantu, muṟṟilum  maṟaintuviṭṭatu. naṭunilakkaṭaṟpakutiyil veppaṅ kuṟainta taṭpaveppa  nilaic cūḻalālum, vaṭaairōppiya, vaṭamēṟku airōppiya makkaḷ toṭarpu  kaḷālum karuniṟak kūṟukaḷ vaḷarcci kuṟaintatu; attuṭaṉ mukaccāyal  kaḷum pēraḷavil oḻuṅkupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
33. airōppāviṉ paḻaṅkaṟkāla ūḻikaḷil nīṇṭanāṭkaḷāka (cakārā,  arēpiyā, pāracikam, turukkistāṉam ākiya iṭaṅkaḷ iṭaikkālaṅ  kaḷiliruntu iṉṟuvarai iruppatupōlirāmal) maṉitavāḻkkaikku mikavum  ukanta cūḻnilai iruntatu. iṭaikkālattil ēṟpaṭṭa immāṟu  talukkukkāraṇam paṉikkaṭṭi ūḻiyiṉ piṟpakutiyil paṉimaṇṭalam  kuṟaintu ataṉāl veppamaṇṭalam vaṭakkunōkkic ceṉṟatēyākum.  ciṟitaḷavu karuniṟakkūṟum nīṇṭatalaivaṭivamum (dolicocephalic)  uṭaiya maṉitaiṉam aipīri yattīvakkuṟai toṭaṅ ki  vaṭaāppirikkā, teṉmēṟku āciyā, teṉintiyā ākiya  iṭaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru perumnilapparappil paravi yiruntaṉar eṉṟu  koḷḷalām.
34. i ṅkē kū ṟiya ta ṭ pave p pa ni lai mā ṟupā ṭ ṭu ṭa ṉ mē ṟ ku  naṭunilakkaṭal pakuti, cūṭāṉiya (hāmittiyaiṉa)p pakuti, cemittiya  (arēpiyāvaic cūḻnta īrappaṇpu tōynta nila)p pakutikaḷ, īrāṉiyap  pakuti, intiyap pakuti ākiyavaṟṟukkiṭaiyē nūṟṟukku nūṟuvarai  illāviṭṭālum, perumaḷavu māṟupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. atumaṭṭumaṉṟi,  mēṟkūṟiyatu pōṉṟa caṟṟuk kavarccimikunta naṉṉilaṅkaḷai nōkki  makkaṭceṟivu paravavum toṭaṅkiṟṟu.

35. itaṉāltāṉ, maṉita iṉak kūṟṟārāycciyāḷarkaḷ naṭunilak  kaṭal iṉam, hāmittiya iṉam, cemittiya iṉam, īrāṉiya iṉam, tirāviṭa  iṉam eṉṟu vakutta pala iṉamā tirikaḷiṉ (racial types)  eccamiccaṅkaḷai mēṟkūṟiya maṇṭalaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilumē kāṇa  muṭikiṟatu. āṉāl atēcamayam ovvoru pakutiyilum avvap pakutiyiṉ  cūḻalukku ukantapaṭi vaḷarcci muṟai nōkkiya muṉṉēṟṟattaiyum  kāṇkiṟōm. itaṉāl ovvōriṭattilum pēraḷavāṉa vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum,  avaṟṟiṉ ūṭāka aṭippaṭaiyil pēraḷavāṉa potu oṟṟumaikaḷaiyum  kāṇkiṟōm. ivvoṟṟumai immaṇṭalattukku veḷiyilum pala  iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭāyiṉum, avaṟṟai nām ippōtu iṅkē kavaṉikkat  tēvaiyillai.
36. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, naṭunilakkaṭal iṉam āppirikka iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ  toṭarpukaḷ uṭaiyatu; hāmittiya iṉattiliruntu atu cila parimāṇaṅ  kaḷil maṭṭumē māṟupaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu. toṭakkattil  māṟupāṭu mikutiyillāta orē cūḻnilaiyil aṭainta vaḷarcci avarkaḷai  aṭippaṭai oṟṟumai uṭaiyavarkaḷākkiviṭṭatu. itupōlavē cemittiya,  īrāṉiya, tirāviṭa allatu intiya iṉaṅkaḷum oṉṟukku oṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāṉa  māṟutalkaḷaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyā inta māṟutal iyakkattil  oru kaṭaikkōṭiyākum. oru puṟam teṉ intiyāviṟkuḷḷēyē inta  māṟutal paṭikaḷpalavaṟṟaip pārkkiṟōm. maṟupuṟam ivaṟṟuṭaṉ mēlai  nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa pala oṟṟumaikaḷaiyum mikat teḷivākak kāṇkiṟōm.
37. putiya kaṟkālat toṭakka mutalāka airōppāvil vāḻkkai  vaḷattukkuriya pala putuppuṉaivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaik  kiṉṟaṉa. putiya ceṭiyiṉam, uyiriṉaṅkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa . itiliruntu  kuṭumpa nilai muṉṉēṟiyirun tateṉṟu m, ku ḻantaikaḷuk kāṉa mel  luṇavukkāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷ toṭaṅkiṉa eṉṟum aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. piṉṉar  maṭpāṇṭakkalai vaḷarntatu . maram veṭṭuvataṟkāṉa āppu vaṭivāṉa kōṭari  tōṉṟiyatu. cakkaraṅkaḷum nākarikattukkuriya atupōṉṟa pala nuṇukkak  karuvi muṟaikaḷum cātaṉaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. paḻaiya karuvikalum tiruntiṉa  nayamāyiṉa. immuṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷ perumpālum pālait  pakutikaḷiliruntu veḷiyēṟiyavar kaḷiṭamē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  palūccistāṉattiṉ kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti ittakaiya puṭaipeyarccikkuriya  maṇṭalam eṉpatai, ataṉ piṟpakutiyil iṉṟum pirākuvi moḻi  eñciyiruppataṉ mūlam uṇaralām.
38. yūpraṭṭis, ṭaikris āṟṟut tāḻ nilaṅkaḷil piṟ  kālaṅkaḷil māperum nākarikaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatai nōkka, intiyāvil  uruvāṉa tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ mītu toṉṟutoṭṭup pāpilōṉiya  nākarikat tākka viḷaivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu  tavaṟaṉṟu.	
39. ki.mu. 800-varai kaṭaṟpōkkuvarattut toṭarpu mikavum kuṟaivē  eṉṟu aṇimaivarai nampappaṭṭiruntatu. āṉāl miṉōvā nāka ri ka m ,  ki rī t nā ka ri ka mū lamāka , a ra ca ma ra pu k ku mu ṟ pa ṭ ṭa ekiptuṭaṉum (predynastic  Egypt) , ki.mu. mūvāyira āṇṭukkuriya cumēruṭaṉum toṭar  puṭaiyatāyiruntatu. attuṭaṉ to l li ya l āy vu mū la m a tu mē ṟ ku  airōppāvu ṭa ṉu m toṭarpuṭaiyatāyiruntatu terikiṟatu. ākavē,  kaṭaṟpōkkuvaravut toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭa kālam ataṟkum neṭuṅkālam  muṟpaṭṭateṉṟum, ki.mu. 3000liruntē mikap pēraḷavāyiruntateṉṟum  kāṇalām.
40. cumēriyap paḻaṅkataikaḷiṉpaṭi, tēvarkaḷ makkaḷukku nākarikap  paṇpāṭṭai mutaṉ mutalil koṭutta poṉṉulakam pāracīka vaḷai  kuṭāviṉai aṭutta ‘tilmuṉ’ (Dilmun) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum cūcā,  mūciyaṉ, ūr, eritu mutaliya muṟpaṭṭa nākarikaṅkaḷukkuriya orē niṟattiṉ  palatiṟac cāyalkaḷamainta, urukkaṇakkiyal (geometry)paṭivaṅkaḷ vāynta  maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, piṟa putukkaṟkāla nākarikap paṇpukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ  taṭayaṅkaḷai, puṣayarttīvil kaṇṭatāka pecārṭu(M.Pezard)kuṟikkiṟār.  taikiris āṟṟu mēl pakutiyai aṭaintapiṉṉar cumēriyar nākarikam  virainta tiṭīr vaḷarcci peṟṟatu pērīntumarac cūḻaliṉ kāraṇamākavē  ākum. avarkaḷiṉ eḻuttup pativukaḷilēyē (cuneiform tablets) “tilmuṉ  īntam paṉaikaḷ niṟainta iṭam” eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭukiṟatu. 
 41. elam, cumēr ākiya iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa vaṇṇa  maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam ki.mu. 4000-kkum muṉṉar celkiṟatu.  cūcāvum atē kālattatē. āṉāl, pērīntukaḷ paṟṟiya vivaram kiṭaippatu  ki.mu.3200 mutal tāṉ. cārkaṉ (Sargon) eṉṟa aracaṉ (ēṟattāḻa  ki.mu.2800-l) caipracuṭaṉ (Cyprus) toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat  terikiṟatu. (kēmpiriṭj paṇṭai varalāṟu. ēṭu1-iyal 10 (1923)) paṇṭai nāṭkaḷil eritu, vakāṣ ākiyavai kaṭaṟkarai nakaraṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. āṉāl  hamurāpiyiṉ kālattiṟkuppiṉ (ki.mu.2100) kaṭal piṉ vāṅki, eritu nakar  ciṟappiḻantatu. nēraṭiyākavē intiyāvukkum mesapoṭṭēmiyāvukkum  nākarikat toṭarpu i ru nta tu eṉ ṟu kāṭṭak kā la va rai yaṟaiyuṭaiya  akaḻāyvuc cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭāviṭṭālum, icceytikaḷāl attoṭarpu  iruntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. (1924 kkuppiṉ cintu nākarika  akaḻāyvuc ceytikaḷ veḷivantu attakaiya toṭarpu meyppikkap  paṭṭuviṭṭatu)
42. ‘muṟkāla nākarikap puṭai peyarcci’ (Migrations of Early Cultures)   eṉṟa nūliṉ āciriyar kirāpṭaṉ eliyaṭ cumit, ‘katiravaṉ  cēykaḷ’ eṉṟa nūliṉ āciriyar ṭapḷyū. jē. periyum viyantu kuṟippiṭum  intiyat tolliyal ārāyccikkūṟu “ulōkak kaṉittaṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ  toṭarpuṭaiyatāka k ku ṟi k ka p peṟu m ka l ci ṉ ṉa ṅ ka ḷ Megaliths  tekkaṇattil kāṇappeṟuvatu” paṟṟiyatu. ikkalmāṭaṅkaḷ orupuṟam  pacipik mākkaṭal pakutiyiluḷḷa itē pōṉṟa kalmāṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉum maṟṟoru  puṟam kāspiyaṉ kaṭalukkut teṟkē jārjiyā, yūksiṉ, kirimiyā, tirēs  ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭupavaṟṟuṭaṉum toṭarpuṭaiyavai eṉṟu  kūṟalām.
43. intak kaṭalvaḻit toṭarpu intiyāvuṭaṉ ki.mu. 3000 liruntē  uṇṭu eṉṟu tiru.peri karutukiṟār. āṉāl māṭakkaṟkaḷiṉ (dolmens)parappu  nōkka nilavaḻit toṭarpaiyum karutalām. tavira “pirākuvi” cāṉṟum  kaṭalvaḻi, nilavaḻi ākiya iru vaḻikaḷukkum potu ātāramākavē uḷḷatu.  iṅṅaṉamāka ēṟkeṉavē nīṭittu nilavi ūṉṟiyirunta mesapeṭṭōmi  yāviṉ uyarnta nākarikattiṉ tākkam ki.mu.2500-kku muṉpu illā  viṭṭālum, kuṟainta aḷavu akkālattilēṉum, tirāviṭa intiyāviṉ mītu  ēṟpaṭṭateṉalām . muṟkālak kaṭalvaḻi vāṇikattuṟaiyil mēṟku, teṉ  n maṟku airōppāvil ettaṉaiyō kaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷil akal  maṇṭaiyōṭu (brachycephalic) maṉitarkaḷaip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm.  ivvakaiyil tiru.hārṉel(Hornell. 1923 livarppūl piriṭṭiṣ caṅka  uraiyil) cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyuḷḷapaṭi intiyāviṉ teṉmēṟku, teṉkiḻakkuk  karaikaḷil akal maṇṭaiyōṭuṭaiya mīṉpaṭavar iruppatai ārāycciyāḷar  kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.
44. muṭivāka, nam kaṇmuṉ ēṟpaṭum karuttōviyam itu: intiyāvil  mu ṟkāla maṉita  i ṉa m  a ḻi vu  paṭā ma l  e ñ ci yi ru n ta tu .  pa ṭi p pa ṭiyāka  varaṇṭuvarum pakutikaḷiliruntu putukkaṟkālammutal naṭunilakkaṭal iṉam,  hāmittiya iṉam, cemittiya iṉam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum palvakai nīṇṭa  talaiyōṭṭu (dolicocephalic) makkaḷ varavāl atu paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcci  peṟṟatu. avarkaḷē perumpālum avvakkālattukkuriya pala karuvi  mēmpāṭukaḷaiyum koṇṭuvantirukkalām. muṟṟilum vēṭarāyirunta  nilaiyiruntu ivai avarkaḷai uyarttiyatuṭaṉ, vēḷāṇmaiyiṉ  toṭakka nilai vaḷarcciyaik kūṭa avaikaḷē ēṟpaṭuttiyirukkak kūṭum.
45. ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukālattil kuṟippiṭat takunta oru potup  paṇpu yāteṉil, neṭuntolaivut toṭarpukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭatākum. itu  intiyāvilum taṭam patikkāmalirukka muṭiyātu. ivvakaiyil mēl  ticaiyuṭaṉ ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpukkuriya mūṉṟu neṟikaḷāvaṉa:
(1) mecapoṭṭēmiyāviliruntu kaṭalvaḻiyākap pāracikakkuṭā,
arāpikkaṭal karaiyōramākavarum neṟi.
(2) mesapoṭṭēmiyāviliruntu intiyā varum nilappātaikaḷ.
(3) eritriyaṉ kaṭal (ceṅkaṭal)cuṟṟup payaṇam (Periplus of the
 Erythrean Sea) eṉṟa nūl vivarittuḷḷapaṭi ceṅkaṭalilum, paṇṭ (Punt)
nāṭṭilum uḷḷa ekiptiyat tuṟaimukaṅkaḷiliruntu varum kaṭalneṟi.
46. āriyar varukaikkuk kuṟaintatu 1000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ  pākavē iṅṅaṉam intiyā vantaṭainta paṇpāṭṭukkūṟukaḷ ēṟkeṉavē  intiyāvil kuṭipukuntirunta maṟṟa uyar iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭik kalappuṟṟaṉa  eṉṟum, tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu akkalappiṉ vaḻi ēṟpaṭṭatē eṉṟum kūṟuvatu  poruntuvatākum.  pērāciriyar puḷūr aṉuppiya piṉ kuṟippu	
47. intak kuṟippukaḷ āciriyar(cilēṭṭar) ārāyccikkuppayaṉ  paṭuttiṭa muṟkuṟippāka mutalil tarappaṭṭiruntatu. piṟaāyvuraikaḷ  aṭippaṭaiyiltāṉ jiyūpiriṭā rikkariyiṉ ‘āciya maṉita iṉak  kūṟṟārāycciyiṉ paruvaraikkōṭukaḷ’ (Outlines of a Systematic Anthropology of Asia: Giufrida Ruggeri).). (kalkattā, 1921) kēmpiriṭj  paṇṭai varalāṟu (pak,2923), maṉita iṉa ārāycci cārnta rāyal kaḻaka itaḻ    (Journal of the Royal Anthropological Institute). (1916, 1918, 1921)
“maṉitaṉ” (Man) eṉṟa itaḻ uḷḷa hec.jē. puḷūr kaṭṭuraikaḷ mutaliyavavaiyē mūlaccāṉṟukaḷākiya appiṟa āyvuraikaḷ ākum.


2. āriyar
intiyap paṇpāṭṭil tirāviṭak kūṟukaḷaic cariyāka matippiṭu  vataṟkup peruntaṭaiyāka uḷḷatu “āriyak kaṭṭukkatai” (Aryan myth)  parappappaṭṭu atuvē cari eṉap palarum(tavaṟākak) karutuvatutāṉ. tām  āriyar, tam camayamum tam nākarikamum āriyac cārpāṉavai eṉṟa  (ātāramaṟṟa) eṇṇattai intiyaruḷ perumpāṉmaiyiṉar ārvak  kōḷāṟiṉāl koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
2 . i n tiyā vi ṉu ḷ va ṭamēṟ ki li ru n tu nuḻai n tu ca m s ki ru ta  moḻiyaik iṅku koṇṭuvantu pukuttiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa poruḷuṭaṉ  ‘āriyar’ eṉṟa peyarai vaḻaṅkiṉāl tavaṟillai. āṉāl māks mūlar  (Max Mueller)peruvaḻakkākap parappiyapaṭi, “maṟṟellā iṉaṅkaḷaiyumviṭa  āriyar nākarika mēmpāṭuṭaiyavarkaḷ; airōppā, āciyāvil eṅkō  iruntatāka uṉṉikkappaṭum āriyattāyakattiliruntu peyarntu pāracikam,  airōppā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilellām kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ; āṅkāṅku vāḻnta  muntaiya makkaḷ aṉaivarum cintaṉai, uṭaṟkūṟu, paṇpāṭu ivaṟṟil  āriyaraiviṭat tāḻntavarkaḷ; avarkaḷait turattiviṭṭu allatu  aṭakkiviṭṭu avviṭaṅkaḷaittamatākkik koṇṭu, avaṟṟai “intō-  airōppiya moḻi”yiṉ kiḷaikaḷāṉa camaskirutam pōṉṟavaṟṟaip  pēcupavar kaḷuk ku aṭimaiyākkiṉar” eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa āriyar eṉṟa  collai vaḻaṅkuvatu nērmaiyaṟṟatu.
3. at tavaṟāṉa karutukōḷai uṇmaic ceytikaḷuṭaṉ porutti  meyppikka valintu ceyta muyaṟcikaḷ takarntuviṭṭaṉa. iṟutiyil iṉam  race eṉpatu moḻi aṭippaṭaiyil maṭṭum amaiyātu eṉa māks mūlarē  ottukkoṇṭār. āyiṉum airōppāvil ikkōṭpāṭu iṉṉum (1924)  muḻumaiyākak kaiviṭappaṭāmal uḷḷatu. hec.ji.vels tam “māntar  varalāṟṟil” (History of mankind) ‘nārṭik āriyar’ (Nordic Aryans)  eṉṟa collai vaḻaṅkukiṟār. āṉāl, āriyar iṉat toṭarpukkūṟukaḷ  etuvāyiṉum, vaṭamēṟku airōppiyarukkuriya taṉit taṉmaikaḷāṉa  nīlaniṟak kaṇkaḷum, veṇṇiṟa mēṉiyum mūla āriyarkaḷ  paṇpāka orukālum iruntirukka muṭiyātu. āṉāl nīlaniṟa  cāmpalniṟak kaṇkaḷai intiyariṭam mēl kaṭaṟkaraiyil maṭṭumē oru  cilariṭam kāṇalām kaṭal vāṇikat toṭarpu kāraṇamāka.
4. intiyāvil āriyak kōṭpāṭu makkaḷatu ātāramaṟṟa uṇarcciyiṉ aṭi ppaṭaiyilēyē i ya ṅ ku kiṉ ṟatu ; tā ṅ ka ḷ āri ya r e ṉ pa ti lu m ,  āriyarilum tāṅkaḷē mūttamarapiṉar eṉpatilum intiyarkaḷ pōlip  perumai koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ‘piriṭṭiṣ, pirañcu, jermaṉ āriyar’ taṅkaḷai  viṭak kuṟaintakāla varalāṟuṭaiya āriyar eṉṟum, mika aṇimaik  kālamvarai avarkaḷ āriyariṉ muḻu nākarika mutircci peṟāta nilaiyiṉa  rākavē iruntaṉar eṉṟum eṇṇit taṟperumai koḷkiṉṟaṉar. muntiya  iyalil kuṟippiṭṭa “tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ paṇṭai iṉattoṭarpukaḷ,  paṇṭai iṉap paṇpāṭṭuk kalappu” ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟi perumpālāṉa  intiyarukku oṉṟumē teriyātu eṉpatāl, tirāviṭarāka evarum  karutappaṭa virumpuvatillai; aṅṅaṉam karutiṉāl taṅkaḷai mēlai  airōppiyaruṭaṉ iṉattoṭarpu aṟṟa maṭṭa iṉamākak karutuvar eṉṟu  añcukiṉṟaṉar!. āriyarākak karutiṉāl, matippuyarvu kiṭaikkumeṉpatu  tavira ikkōṭpāṭṭukku vēṟu ātāram illai.  5. aṇimaiyil mayars (Myres) kutirai paṟṟiya tam varalāṟṟārāyc  ciyil kēmpriṭj paṇṭaivaralāṟu ēṭu 1 : pakkam-106-7. āriyar paraval,  ataṉ kālam, avarkaḷ aṭippaṭaip paṇpukaḷ ākiyavai paṟṟi virivāka  viḷakkiyuḷḷār. “paḻaṅkālamutal uṇavukkāka kutirai vēṭṭaiyāṭappaṭṭu  vantuḷḷatu. āṉāl, naṭu āciya mēṭṭu nilattukku veḷiyē, eṅkum  pāltarum vīṭṭu vilaṅkākakkūṭa atu paḻakkap paṭuttappaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkāṉa  cāṉṟukaḷ etuvum kiṭṭavillai. “airōppāvil ‘hālsṭaṭ’  Halstatt   pakutiyil vaḻaṅkiya nākarikam tāṉ “ kutiraiyai ēṟiyūrvataṟ kum  vaṇṭiyiḻuppataṟkum oruṅkē payaṉpaṭuttiya” mutal nākarikam ākum.  āciyāvil itaṟkāṉa mutal teḷivāṉa cāṉṟu, ki.mu. 2100-kkuriya oru  pāpilōṉiyap kaḷimaṇ ōṭṭup poṟippēyākum. atu, ‘kīḻtticaikkuriya,  malaippakutikkuriya, kaḻutaikaḷ’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. atuvaraik  kaḻutai maṭṭumē vaḻaṅkivanta jākrās malaittoṭarukku mēṟkēyuḷḷa  makkaḷukku atu aṉṟu putiya paḻakkamākavē iruntirukkiṟatu. 
 6. kutiraiyiṉ varavu īrāṉiliruntum, ataṟkappāliruntum tiraṇṭu paṭaiyeṭuttu vantu, ki.mu.1750-il pāpilōṉiṉ ‘kācaiṭ’ Kassite marapai niṟuviya putiya makkaḷukkuriyatākavē kuṟikkappeṟukiṟatu. mullai nilanākarikattait tāṉākap piṟappittu vaḷarkkum taṉmai īrāṉiya mēṭṭu nilattil iṉṟum illai, aṉṟum iruntirukka vāyppillaieṉavē, mēṟcoṉṉa paṭaiyeṭuppu iṉṉum caṟṟu vaṭakiḻakkiliruntu, atāvatu carmēṣiyaṉ(Sarmatian) camaveḷiyiliyuntē toṭaṅ ki yi rukkalām. camakālattil i llāviṭṭālum mukkiyattuvattil atē vaṭaticait  tāyakattiliruntu(piṉṉar) intiyāvukkup puṭaipeyarntu vanta āriyamoḻi  pēciya makkaḷ varavuṭaṉ atu toṭarpuṭaiyatē. airōppāvil nīppar āṟu  kaṭantu kaliṣiyā (Galicia) vaḻiyāka pohīmiyāvukkum, pālkaṉ tīvakkuṟai  vaḻiyākac ciṟiya āciyāvukkum ceṉṟa puṭaippeyarccikaḷuṭaṉum itaṉait  toṭarpupaṭuttalām.
7. eṉavē, āriyarkaḷ maṟṟa iṉaṅkaḷai nōkka nākarikam  kuṉṟiyavarkaḷ(barbaric invaders)tām eṉiṉum viraintu,  mikappalar paṭaiyeṭukkac cātakamāṉa kutiraikaḷuṭaṉ vantirukka  vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutalām. kutiraivīrarkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti araciyal  ātikkam peṟṟaṉar. cumēriyarum tirāviṭarum celvam, nākarikam  pōṉṟavaṟṟil mēmpaṭṭiruntālum, āṟṟalum veṟṟiyum tarum kutiraip  paṭai avarkaḷiṭam illai; caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. avar  kaḷuṭaiya tiraṇṭa celvamum pakaivarkaḷai īrttatu. kutirai maṭṭumiṉṟi  pala putumaikaḷaiyum (iṟantōrai eriyūṭṭum vaḻakkam, ataṉuṭaṉ  toṭarpuṭaiyatāṉa “iṟappukkup piṉ maṟuulakil uyarnilai” paṟṟiya  kōṭpāṭu mutaliyavai) koṇarntaṉar. ivai tirāviṭaruṭaiya paḻaiya  nampikkaikaḷai aḻikkāviṭiṉum uṭaṉ kalantuviṭṭaṉa.  
8. kutirai payaṉpaṭṭa nākarikam, kutiraikku muṟpaṭṭa kāla nākarikam  ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷai oppiṭum kirēkka purāṇak katai oṉṟu  uṇṭu. tēvaṉ pāciṭāṉum (Poseidon) tēvi atēṉāvum, atēṉs  (Athens) nakarattiṟku yār peyarai vaippatu eṉpatu paṟṟip pōṭṭiyiṭṭaṉar.  yār tarum naṉkoṭai matippuyarvaṭaiyatō, avar peyaraiyē ēṟpateṉṟu  ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. pāciṭāṉ kutiraiyaik koṭuttār. atēṉā  tēvatāru marattai (Olive tree). aḷittār. tēvarkaḷ naṭuvar kuḻu atēṉā  pakkamē tīrppaḷittatu. ēṉeṉil, atēṉāviṉ naṉkoṭai amaiti  cārntatu. pāciṭāṉiṉ naṉkoṭaiyō pōrukkē payaṉpaṭuvatu.  itaiyotta kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl ekiptiya nākarikattai hiksō  nākarikattukkum, rōmaṉ-kirēkka nākarikattai ataṉai vīḻttiya turukkiya  nākarikattukkum mēmpaṭṭatāka karutalāmāṉāl, āriyap paṭaiyeṭuppuk  kāla tirāviṭa nākarikattai āriya nākarikattukku mēmpaṭṭatākak   karutat taṭaiyillai!
9. intiya nākarikattaip poṟuttum itupōlavē, (iṉṉum  mukkiyamāka tirāviṭa āriya nākarikakkūṟukaḷ enta viḻukkāṭṭaḷavil  oṉṟukūṭiyiṇaintuḷḷaṉa eṉṉum vakaiyil) nām naṭunilaiyuṭaṉ  ārāyavēṇṭum. muṟkāla kirēkka varalāṟṟiṉ oppumai itaṟkut  tuṇaiceyvatu ākum. āṉāl, oppumai muṟṟilum cariyāṉatāka irukka  vēṇṭumāyiṉ, nām ataṉaic caṟṟu māṟupaṭuttik kaṟpaṉaiceytu  pārkka vēṇṭum. nākarikamaṟṟa āriya, kirēkka muraṭarkaḷ paṭaiyeṭuppukaḷ  miṉōva, mīciṉiya nākarikaṅkaḷai muṟṟilum aḻittaṉa eṉpatu varalāṟu.  āṉāl “aṅṅaṉam aḻikkāmal korint vaḷaikuṭāvaik kaṭantu  ammuraṭarkaḷ cellavillai e ṉ ṟu m ; pelappoṉes tī va k kuṟai  maṭṭum taṉ paṇṭaiya moḻiyaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum kāttu nilaipeṟa  vaittukkoṇṭatu” eṉṟum (naṭakkāta oṉṟai) nām kaṟpaṉai ceytāl,  kirēkka nāṭṭu varalāṟu evvāṟu iruntirukkum eṉṟu nām eṇṇip  pārkka vēṇṭum!
10. intiyāvukku nēril vanta veḷināṭṭāḷar palar muṭivukaḷil oru  muraṇpāṭṭaik kāṇalām. intiya makkaḷil perumpālōriṉ  aṭippaṭai iṉa oṟṟumai kaṇṭu viyappaṭaivatuṭaṉ intiyar  peritum iṉattāl tirāviṭarē eṉṉum cariyāṉa muṭivaiyum ēṟṟuk  koḷkiṟārkaḷ; āyiṉum atēcamayam intiya nākarikam, camayam,  meyviḷakkat tattuvaṅ kaḷ ākiyavai aṉaittum āriyac  cārpāṉavai! eṉṉum pāmarat taṉamāṉa kōṭpāṭṭaiyum āḻnta  cintaṉaiyiṉṟi ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivvakaiyilēyē, “ñāyiṟum  nākamum” (The Sun and the Serpant) eṉṟa ciṟanta nūlil ci.ep.ōlṭuhām  (Oldham) vaṭaintiyap potu makkaḷiṉ tirāviṭa iṉaccārpai eṭuttuk  kāṭṭiviṭṭu, ataṉpiṉṉarum pirāmaṇa matam āriyar uruvākkiyatu eṉṟum;  nāka (tirāviṭa) aracāṭciyilum ‘pirāmaṇarē ātikka nilaiyil iruntaṉar’  eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyam kuṟippatālēyē, “anta aracukaḷ attaṉaiyum  āriyamayamākiviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu karutalām” eṉkiṟār. āyiṉum pirāmaṇa  cātiyil uccanilai uyarvum cari, cāti vēṟupāṭum cari, ivaṟṟiṉ  aṭippaṭaiyil avarkaḷai āriya marapu eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkuc ciṟitēṉum  ātāram illai. māksmūlar (ciṟu tuṇukkukaḷ ( Chips) ēṭu:2 pakkam-   311) kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
“vētaṅkaḷiṉ mikap paḻamaiyāṉa camayat tattuvaṅkaḷil ma ṉu  kuṟippatum iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷatumāṉa cātimuṟai iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatā?  “illai” eṉṟa orē collil nām atai aḻuttamāka maṟuttu  viṭa lām.peru ñci kka l vā ynta cāti amaip pu muṟait  tiṭṭattukku vēta cūktaṅkaḷil ettakaiya ātāramum illai,  atupōlavē cūttirariṉ iḻitakai nilaimaikku ātāramō; palvēṟu  vakuppiṉar oruṅkē kuḻumi vāḻa, oruṅkē uṇṇap parukat taṭai vitikkum  caṭṭamō; palvēṟu cātiyiṉar tammuḷ oruvarukkoruvar maṇa uṟavu  koḷvatait taṭukkum muṟaimaiyō; attakaiya maṇa uṟavālvarum  piḷḷaikaḷukku vilakka muṭiyāta iḻivukkuṟiyiṭṭut tīṇṭattakātava  rāka otukki vaikkum kaṭṭuppāṭō; etuvum avaṟṟil illai.  attuṭaṉ civaṉ, kāḷi ākiyavarkaḷiṉ accantarum ceyalmuṟaikaḷ;  kaṇṇaṉiṉ ciṟṟiṉpak kaḷiyāṭṭam; tirumāliṉ teyvīka aruñceyalkaḷ,  ivai paṟṟi, vētattil oṉṟum illai. ‘kaṭavuḷukkuriya matippait  tamateṉak koṇṭu tammaiyē paḻi k ku ḷḷā k kik koḷḷu m o ru  ku rumār  ku ḻu vi ṉ vī m purimaika ḷ ’, “ma ṉi ta i ṉa t ti ṉ kiḷaikaḷai  vilaṅkilum kīḻāka iḻivupaṭuttum muṟai” ākiyavaṟṟai ātarikkum entac  caṭṭamum avaṟṟil illai. kuḻantai maṇa ātaravu, vitavai maṟumaṇat  taṭai, kaṇavaṉ piṇattuṭaṉ kaimpeṇṇai uyiruṭaṉ erikkum  koṭumai ivaṟṟai ātarittu vētattil oru vācakaṅkūṭa illai. ivai  yāvum vētattiṉ tattuvattukkum collukkum māṟupaṭṭavai.”
11. cātimuṟai; pirāmaṇar teyvikattaṉmai, kāḷi, civaṉ, tirumāl,  civaṉ tuṇaivi pārvati, civaṉ makaṉkaḷ murukaṉ, kaṇapati, tirumāliṉ  iṟuti avatāramāṉa kaṇṇaṉ, ākiyōr vaḻipāṭu - ittaṉaiyum veḷi  yiliruntu vantavai, “āriyar vēta aṭippaṭaiyilāṉa” intiya nākarikat  tuṭaṉ oṭṭik koṇṭa mācukaḷ eṉṟum otukkiviṭa muṭiyātu. avai  intiyap paṇpāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭai uyir nilaikkūṟukaḷ ākum. kāḷi,  civaṉ, tirumāl, mutaliyavarkaḷ vētatteyvaṅkaḷum allar;  āriyaruṭaiyavarumalla eṉpatu teḷivu. avarkaḷait tirāviṭar kaṭavuḷkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavira vēṟu vaḻi illai.
